aṉṟiyum, vaṭu nīṅkiya ciṟappup poruntiya taṉ maṉiya kattiyum maṟantoḻintāṉ.
4. antimālic ciṟappuccey kāti
kōvalaṉ taṉ maṉiyakatti maṟantu, mātaviyiṉ maṉik kaṇṇē mayaṅkik kiṭakka nāṭkaḷ pala kaḻintoḻintaṉa. taṉṉi avaṉ piriyāmal oḻukuvatāl mātavikku makiḻvu mikuvatāyiṟṟu; avaṉ pirivāṟṟāta kaṇṇakikku uṇṭāṉa tuyar aḷapparitā yiṟṟu. kātalarip pirintavarkku mālippōtu poṟuttaṟkariya tuṉpam taruvatātalāl, oru nāḷiya antimāliyiṉ ciṟappu iṅkē kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
pakaṟpoḻutu kaḻiya antimāli vantatu. pakalavaṉ mēlit ticiyil maṟintāṉ. eṅkum pacuveyil parantatu; māliyil malarum pūkkaḷiṭattē paṉinīr taṅkit tuḷikkaluṟṟatu; eṅkum kuḷircci nilavat toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
“virikatir parappi ulakamuḻu tāṇṭa
1orutaṉit tikiri uravōṟ kāṇēṉ;
aṅkaṇ vāṉattu aṇinilā virikkum
tiṅkaḷañ celvaṉ yāṇṭuḷaṉkol?” eṉat
ticimukam pacantu, 2cemmalark kaṇkaḷ
3muḻunīr vāra, muḻumeyum paṉittut,
4tirinīr āṭi irunila maṭanti,
5aricu keṭuttu alamarum 6allaṟ kāliyē
ivvanti māliyākum. ciṟitu pōtil 1mayaṅkiruḷ varuva tāyiṟṟu.
2kaṟikeḻu kuṭikaḷ kitali vippa
3aṟipōku kuṭikaḷoṭu orutiṟam paṟṟi
4valampaṭu tāṉi maṉṉar ilvaḻip
5pulampaṭa iṟutta 6viruntiṉ maṉṉariṉ
7tāḻtuṇi tuṟantōr 8taṉittuyar eytak
kātalarp puṇarntōr kaḷimakiḻ veytak
9cūḻalvaḷar mulliyil kōvalar tammoṭu
10maḻalit tumpi vāyvit tūta;
11aṟukāṟ kuṟumpeṟintu arumpupoti vācam
12ciṟukāṟ celvaṉ maṟukil tūṟṟa
13elvaḷi makaḷir maṇiviḷak keṭuppa
14mallal mūtūr mālivan tiṟuttatu.
inniliyil teṉṉavar kula mutalāṉa piṟit tiṅkaḷ tōṉṟiyatu. pakivar iḷiyarāyiṉum avari muḷiyilē kaḷiyum aracu muṟi at teṉṉavarkku iyalpāka uṇṭu; ataṉāl, at teṉṉar kulat tiṅkaḷ, anti mālip pōtil aṭinta mayaṅkiruḷi māṟṟip pāl pōṉṟa taṉ veṇ katirip parappiṟṟu. nilavup payaṉ koḷḷum neṭu nilā muṟṟattil, mātavi, kōvalaṉi etirēṟṟu, avaṉukkuk kalaviyum pulaviyum nalkik kaḷi koṇṭaṉaḷ. mātaviyip pōlavē kātalarik kūṭiya makaḷirum 1ceḻumpūñ cēkkik kaṇṇē,
manta mārutattu mayaṅkiṉar 2malintāṅku
3āviyaṅ koḻunar akalat toṭuṅkik
4kāviyaṅ kaṇṇāl kaḷittuyil eyta,
tam maṉam poruntiya kātalarik kūṭutaṟkiṉṟit tamit tirukkum makaḷir cilar peruntuyar uḻantaṉar. avaruḷ kaṇṇaki,
amceñ cīṟaṭi aṇicilam poḻiya
meltukil 5alkul mēkali nīṅka,
koṅki muṉṟil kuṅkumam eḻutāḷ;
maṅkala aṇiyiṉ piṟitaṇi makiḻāṉ;
6koṭuṅkuḻi tuṟantu 7vaṭintuvīḻ kātiṉaḷ;
tiṅkaḷ vāḷmukam ciṟuviyar piriyac
8ceṅkayal neṭuṅkaṇ añcaṉam maṟappap
9pavaḷa vāynutal tilakam iḻappa,
tavaḷa vāḷ naki kōvalaṉ iḻappa
10miyiruṅ kūntal neyyaṇi maṟappak
kiyaṟum neñcamoṭu kalakkam uṟṟaṉaḷ. avaḷi otta ēṉi makaḷir, 1ūtulik kuruku pōla veytuyirttu, vēṉiṟ kālattu eytum paḷḷiyiṭatti mēvātu kuḷir kālattup paḷḷiyi mēviṉar; aṅkē, nilāvoḷiyum teṉṟalum pukāvaṇṇam 2kuṟuṅ kaṇ cāḷaraṅkaḷi aṭittuk koṇṭu oṭuṅkik kiṭantaṉar. kātalarik kūṭiya kālamellām kalaṅki yaṟiyāta avar uḷḷam, itupōtu kalaṅkuvatāyiṟṟu; kaṭik kaṇṇum civappatāyiṟṟu. ākavē; avarkaḷ naḷḷiruḷ yāmattum pakalum tuñcātu varuntiṉar. kāmaṉum malarampum karumpu villum ēnti nakark kāval puriyalāṉāṉ.
ivvaṇṇam iravup pōtu kaḻitalum, ataṉ iṟutiyākiya viṭiyaṟ kālam,
aṉṉa melnaṭi naṉṉīrp poyki
3āmpal nāṟum tēmpoti naṟuvirit
tāmaric cevvāyt 4taṇṇaṟal kūntal
pāṇvāy vaṇṭu 5nōtiṟam pāṭak
6kāṇvaru kuvaḷik kaṇmalar viḻippap
puḷvāy muracamoṭu 7poṟimayir vāraṇattu
8muḷvāyc caṅkam muṟimuṟi ārppa
9uravunīrp parappiṉ ūrtuyil eṭuppi eytiṟṟu.
5. intira viḻa ūreṭutta kāti
1alinī rāṭi malimuli yākattu
ārap pēriyāṟṟu mārik kūntal
2kaṇṇakaṉ parappiṉ 3maṇṇaka maṭanti
4putiyiruṭ paṭāam pōka nīkki
5utiya mālvari uccit tōṉṟi
ulakuviḷaṅku 6aviroḷi malarkatir parappi
veyil viḷaṅkum naṇpakaṟ pōtiṉic ceyya, cittiri mātattuc cittirā pūraṇi vantatu. an nāḷ toṭaṅki irupat teṭṭu nāḷkāṟum kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattil intiraṉukku viḻā ayarvatu vaḻakkam. ak kālattē, 7vēyāmāṭam, 8viyaṉkala irukki, māḷiki iṭaṅkaḷ mutalākap 9perumpāṇirukki, piṟa 10ciṟu kuṟuṅki viṉiyāḷar irukki īṟākap poruntiya maruvūrp pākkamum, kōviyal vīti, koṭittēr vītikaḷ mutalāka, kutiri, kaḷiṟu, tēr eṉṟa ivaṟṟiṉ pākarum, pōr vīrarum irukkum 11perumpā yirukki, pīṭukeḻu tiruviṉ periyōr irukki īṟākavuḷḷa paṭṭiṉap pākkamum eṉa iru pakutip paṭa iruntatu ik kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉam. ivviru pākkaṅkaṭkum iṭiyē palvaki maram ceṟinta cōli yoṉṟu uṇṭu. am maraṅkaḷiṉ aṭiyil kaṭikaḷ kaṭṭap peṟṟiruntaṉa. avviṭam nāḷaṅkāṭi eṉpatu.
in nāḷaṅkāṭiyil, kāvaṟ pūtattup pali pīṭiki, uyirppali vaḻaṅkum muḻuppali pīṭiki, koṟṟap pantar, paṭṭi maṇṭapam, tōraṇa vāyil, uṇarntōr maṇṭapam, veḷḷiṭi maṉṟam, ilañci maṉṟam, neṭuṅkal maṉṟam, pūta catukkam, pāvi maṉṟam mutaliya pala cīriya iṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa.
intiraviḻā ayartaṟkuriya cittiri cērnta cittirit tiṅkaḷil uyarntōrkaḷ oṉṟu kūṭi,
meyvaki yuṇarnta 1viḻumiyōr ēttum
2aivaki maṉṟattum arumpali uṟīi
3vaccirak kōṭṭattu maṇaṅkeḻu muracam
4kacci yāṉip piṭarttali ēṟṟi
5vālveṇ kaḷiṟṟaracu vayaṅkiya kōṭṭattuk
6kālkōḷ viḻaviṉ 7kaṭinili cāṟṟit
taṅkiya koḷkit 8tarunili kōṭṭattu
maṅkala neṭuṅkoṭi vāṉuṟa eṭuttu
vētiki, neṭunili māḷiki mutaliyaṉa poruntiya terukkaḷit tōraṇam nāṟṟi, pūraṇa kumpam, poṟ pāliki, pāvi viḷakku, poṟ paṭāki, kavari, cuṇṇam mutaliyavaṟṟāl aḻakuṟuttiṉar. piṉpu, aimperuṅ kuḻuvum, 9eṇpērāyamum, araca kumararum, 1parata kumararum, puravi, kaḷiṟu, tēr eṉṟa ivaṟṟiṉ mēl ivarntu vantu oruṅku kūṭi,
aricu 2mēmpaṭīiya 3akalnili maruṅkiṉ
4uricāl maṉṉaṉ 5koṟṟam koḷkeṉa,
māyiru ñālattu maṉṉuyir kākkum
āyirat tōreṭ 6ṭaracu talik koṇṭa
taṇnaṟuṅ kāvirit tātumali peruntuṟip
puṇṇiya naṉṉīr poṟkuṭat tēnti
7maṇṇakam maruḷa vāṉakam viyappa
viṇṇavar talivaṉi viḻunī rāṭṭi
viḻā ayaralāyiṉar. orupakkattē,
8piṟavā yākkip periyōṉ kōyilum,
aṟumukac cevvēḷ aṇitikaḻ kōyilum,
9vālvaḷi mēṉi vāliyōṉ kōyilum,
nīla mēṉi neṭiyōṉ kōyilum,
10māli veṇkuṭi maṉṉavaṉ kōyilum
ākiya ik kōyilkaḷil nāṉmaṟiyiṟ kūṟiya muṟiyil tappātu tī vaḷarkkap peṟṟaṉa. oru pakkattē, nāl vakit tēvar, patiṉeṇ kaṇaṅkaḷ eṉṟa vēṟu vēṟu kaṭavuḷarkkum viḻāc ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. aṟavōr paḷḷiyil aṟavuriyum, puṇṇiyat tāṉaṅkaḷil tiṟavōr urikkum naṟceyalum *nikaḻntaṉa; oru pāl, ciṟippaṭṭa maṉṉaratu aṭittaḷi nīkkic 1ciṟivīṭu ceyyum aruṭ ceyal nikaḻntatu; orupakkattē, 2kaṇṇuḷar, 3kuyiluvar, yāḻppulavar, pāṭaṟpāṇar mutaliyōr eṇṇaruñ ciṟappuṭaṉ iciyicittuc ciṟantaṉar; iravum pakalum māṭavīti, kuṟunteru, mūlimuṭukkar eṉṟa ellā iṭaṅkaḷilum muḻavu muḻakkam iṭiyaṟātu intira viḻā iṉitu naṭi peṟṟatu. ivvaṇṇam.
viḻavukkaḷi ciṟanta 4viyaluḷāṅkaṇ,
kātaṟ koḻunaṉip pirintu alareytā
5mātark koṭuṅkuḻi mātavi taṉṉōṭu
kāmakkaḷi makiḻveyti, naṟuvirip poḻilil viḷi yāṭṭamarntu, nāḷaṅkāṭiyiluḷḷa pūmali kāṉattup putu maṇam nukarntu, pukiyum cāntum koṇṭu, nakiyāṭāyattāriṉ naṉmoḻiyil tiḷittuk kuralvāyp pāṇar, nakarap parattar eṉṟa ivaroṭu kūṭit tirintaṉaṉ kōvalaṉ; ivaṉippōla iḷavēṉiṟ paruvattu iṉitu varum teṉṟal, māṭavītikaḷil maṇaṅ kamaḻntu ulaviṟṟu.
iṅṅaṉam mālippōtu kaḻiyavē, naḷḷiravu eytiṟṟu. ak kālip potumakaḷir uṟiyum terukkaḷilum kulamakaḷir uṟiyum terukkaḷilum muṟiyē kāmukar pukaḻcciyum illaṟa vuyarcciyum eḻuntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, kāmukar,
‘irunila maṉṉaṟkup peruvaḷam kāṭṭat
tirumakaḷ pukuntatuic ceḻumpati yām’ eṉa
6eriniṟat tilavamum mulliyum aṉṟiyum
karuneṭuṅ kuvaḷiyum 7kumiḻum pūttuāṅku
1uḷvarik kōlattu uṟutuṇi tēṭik
2kaḷḷak kamalam tiritalum uṇṭukol!
maṉṉavaṉ ceṅkōl maṟutta lañcip
palluyir parukum 3pakuvāyk kūṟṟam
āṇmiyil tirintutaṉ 4aruntoḻil tiriyātu
nāṇuṭik kōlattu nakimukam 5kōṭṭip
paṇmoḻi narampiṉ 6tivavuyāḻ miḻaṟṟip
peṇmiyil tiriyum peṟṟiyum uṇṭukol!
eṉap potumakaḷirip pukaḻntu kūṟi makiḻntaṉar. ivvāṟu potu makaḷiri ūṭal tīrttuk kūṭiya āṭavar. avar pūciya cāntum, cūṭiya māliyum kūṭiya kuṟiyumuṭi yarāyt tam maṉikkaṇ, viḻaviṟku vanta viruntiṉarōṭu ceṉṟaṉar. avar ceyal kaṇṭu pulattaṟkuriya avartam kulamakaḷir pulattaliṉum vanta viruntōmpal pēraṟam eṉṟu uṭkoṇṭu, avarōṭu uṭaṉuṟivu maruvukiṉṟaṉar. ataṉi aṟintu makiḻum avartam koḻunar,
viruntoṭu pukka peruntōḷ kaṇavaroṭu
7uṭaṉuṟivu marīi oḻukkoṭu puṇarnta
vaṭamīṉ kaṟpiṉ maṉiyuṟi makaḷir
1mātarvāṇ mukattu 2maṇittōṭṭuk kuvaḷip
pōtu 3puṟaṅkoṭuttup pōkiya 4ceṅkaṭi
5viruntiṉ tīrntila tāyiṉ yāvatum
maruntum taruṅkolim mānila varippueṉa
maṉamārap pārāṭṭiṉar. inta naḷḷiravil,
6uḷḷakam naṟuntātu uṟippa mītaḻintu
kaḷḷuka naṭuṅkum kaḻunīr pōlak
kaṇṇaki 7karuṅkaṇum mātavi 8ceṅkaṇum
uḷniṟi karantu akattoḷittu nīr ukuttaṉa;
eṇṇumuṟi iṭattiṉum valattiṉum tuṭittaṉa.
6. kaṭalāṭu kāti
 veḷḷimāl variyiṉ vaṭapakutiyil cēṭi eṉṉum vittiyātara nakaramoṉṟu uṇṭu. āṅku vāḻum viñciyaruḷ oruvaṉ, kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattil intira viḻā naṭipeṟum kālatti aṟintu av viḻā nikaḻcciyit taṉ kātalikkuk kāṭṭa virumpiṉāṉ. ataṉāl, avaṉ taṉ kātaliyi nōkki, “tuṭiyiṭiyāy!
teṉtici maruṅkiṉ 1ceḻumpati taṉṉuḷ
intiraviḻavu koṇṭu eṭukkunāḷ itu”
eṉac colli, “āṅkē nām celvōm varuka; mēlum, ac ceḻumpatiyil,
aivaki maṉṟat tamitiyum kāṇkutum;
2nārataṉ vīṇi nayanteri pāṭalum,
tōriya maṭanti vāram pāṭalum,
3maṅki mātavi vaḻimutal tōṉṟiya
aṅkara valkul āṭalum kāṇkutum;
amarar talivaṉi vaṇaṅkutum yām” eṉa,
avaḷum ataṟku icintaṉaḷ. iruvarum puṟappaṭṭu varuṅkāl, avaṉ avaḷukku, imaya variyum kaṅkiyāṟum, uñcippatiyum, vintamalik kāṭum, vēṅkaṭa maliyum, kāviri nāṭum kāṭṭi, pukār nakarkkaṇ pukuntu amarar talivaṉi vaṇaṅki nakariṭi eṭukkum viḻā nalam kāṇaluṟṟāṉ. iruvarum tam viñciyar uruvi maṟittukkoṇṭu, an nakarkkaṇ nikaḻnta kūttum pāṭṭum kāṇpārāyiṉar.
1tēvapāṇi yeṉṉum icippāṭṭu vakiyip pāṭi,
umiyavaḷ orutiṟa ṉāka ōṅkiya
imiyava ṉāṭiya 2koṭukoṭṭi yāṭalum,
pārati yāṭiya viyaṉ 3pāṇ ṭaraṅkamum,
añcaṉa vaṇṇaṉ āṭiya āṭaluḷ
4alliyat tokutiyum, avuṇaṟ kaṭanta
5malliṉ āṭalum, mākkaṭal naṭuvaṇ
cūrttiṟam kaṭantōṉ āṭiya 6tuṭiyum,
paṭivīḻt tavuṇar 7piyuḷ eytak
kuṭivīḻttu avarmuṉ āṭiya 8kuṭiyum,
nīḷnila maḷantōṉ āṭiya 9kuṭamum,
kāma ṉāṭiya 10pēṭi yāṭalum
1māyava ḷāṭiya 2marakkā lāṭalum
tiruviṉ ceyyōḷ āṭiya 3pāviyum
4ayirāṇi maṭanti āṭiya 5kaṭiyamum
ākiya ivaṟṟit taṉ kātalikkuk kāṭṭiṉa av viñciyaṉ, āṭaṟkōlattōṭu mātavi tōṉṟak kaṇṭu,
“tātaviḻ pūmpoḻil iruntuyāṉ kūṟiya
6mātavi marapiṉ mātavi ivaḷ” eṉak
kātalik kurittuk kaṇṭumakiḻ veytiya
kālattē, kōvalaṉ, intira viḻā muṭivu peṟutalālum, mātavi āṭaliṟ kālam nīṭṭittalālum, palarum avaḷip parivāyp pārttalāl vanta poṟāmiyālum veṟuppuṟṟu ūṭaṟ kōlattōṭu iruntāṉ. avaṉ uvakkumāṟu, mātavi kōlaṅ koḷḷalāṉāḷ: mātavi taṉ kūntalil naṟu ney taṭavi nalam ceytu koṇṭāḷ. civanta ciṟiya aṭikaḷil cempañci yūṭṭappeṟṟaṉa; mel viralkaḷil nalla aṇikaḷ ceṟintaṉa; tuṭiyil 7kuṟaṅku ceṟi, eṉṉum pūṇ aṇiyap peṟṟatu. kālukkup pariyakam, nūpuram, pāṭakam, cataṅki, pātacālam mutaliyaṉa aṇiyappaṭṭaṉa; cīriya pūntukil uṭuttu, tōḷukkuk kaṇṭikiyōṭu piṉṉiya 8tōḷvaḷi ceṟittuk koṇṭāḷ; muṉkikkuc cūṭakamum, melviraṟku maṇiyāḻiyum, kaḻuttiṟku muttāramum tūmaṇik kōviyum, kātiṟkuk kaṭippiṇiyum kaviṉuṟa aṇintukoṇṭāḷ.
ivvāṟu taṉṉi oppaṉi ceytu koṇṭavaḷ, viḻā nāḷ muṭintapiṉ vanta 1uvānāḷil kaṭalāṭaṟku makkaḷ mikka āravārattōṭu viriyak kaṇṭāḷ; uṭaṉē, avaḷ,
2perunīr pōkum 3iriyal mākkaḷoṭu
maṭalaviḻ kāṉal kaṭalviḷi yāṭṭuk
kāṇṭal viruppoṭu vēṇṭiṉa ḷāki,
poykit tāmari 4puḷvāy pulampa
vikaṟi yāmam vāraṇam kāṭṭa
5veḷḷi viḷakkam naḷḷiruḷ kaṭiyat
tāraṇi mārpaṉoṭu pēraṇi yaṇintu
avaṉ kōvēṟu kaḻutimītuvara, tāṉ oru vaṇṭiyilēṟi paṭṭiṉappākkattu māṭamali maṟukum pīṭikit teruvum, tirumakaḷ nīṅkātuṟiyum āvaṇa vītiyum kaṭantu, maruvūrp pākkattu nakara vītiyiṉ naṭuvē ceṉṟu, paratēcamakkaḷ vāḻum 6mālic cēriyiṉ   maruṅkē aṭintāḷ. aṅkē,
vaṇṇamum cāntum malarum cuṇṇamum
7paṇṇiyap pakutiyum pakarvōr viḷakkamum,

8kāḻiyar mōtakat tūḻuṟu viḷakkamum,
9kūviyar kārakaṟ kuṭakkāl viḷakkamum,
10noṭinavil makaṭūuk kaṭikeḻu viḷakkamum,
iṭiyiṭi mīṉvili pakarvōr viḷakkamum,
1ilaṅkunīr varippiṉ 2kalaṅkari viḷakkamum,

kaṭṭuri
(eṉṟu mātavi pāṭak kēṭṭu), yāḻ ici mēl vittut taṉ ūḻviṉi vantu uruttatākaliṉ, (kōvalaṉ) yāṉ kāṉal vari pāṭa, (ivaḷ) tāṉ oṉṟiṉ mēl maṉam vittup pāṭiṉāḷ; (ivaḷ) 1māyap poy pala kūṭṭum māyattāḷ eṉa, uvavuṟṟa tiṅkaḷ mukattā(ḷākiya avaḷik) 2kavavukki nekiḻntaṉaṉāy, “īṅkum poḻutu kaḻintatu; ātaliṉ, eḻutum” eṉṟu (ava)ḷuṭaṉ eḻātu, (tāṉmaṭṭil) ēvalāḷar uṭaṉcūḻtarap (pōyiṉaṉ;) pōṉa piṉṉar, tātaviḻ malarccōli 3otiyāyttu oliyavittuk kiyaṟṟa neñciṉaḷāy viyattiṉ uḷpukkuk kātalaṉuṭaṉ aṉṟiyē mātavi taṉ maṉipukkāḷ.
8. vēṉiṟ kāti
“1neṭiyōṉ kuṉṟamum 2toṭiyōḷ puvamum
tamiḻvaram paṟutta taṇpuṉal naṉṉāṭṭu
māṭa maturiyum pīṭār 3uṟantiyum
4kalikeḻu vañciyum olipuṉal pukārum
aricuvīṟ ṟirunta uricāl ciṟappiṉ
maṉṉaṉ 5māraṉ makiḻtuṇi yākiya
iṉiḷa vēṉil vantaṉaṉ ivaṇ” eṉa
vaḷamkeḻu potiyil māmuṉi payanta
6iḷaṅkāl tūtaṉ icittaṉaṉ; ātaliṉ,
7makara velkoṭi mintaṉ cēṉi
pukaraṟu kōlam koḷḷumeṉ patupōl,
koṭimiṭi cōlik 8kuyilōṉ eṉṉum
9paṭiyuḷ paṭuvōṉ 10paṇimoḻi kūṟa,
maṭalaviḻ kāṉaṟ kaṭalviḷi yāṭṭiṉuḷ
kōvalaṉ ūṭak, kūṭātu ēkiya
māmalar neṭuṅkaṇ mātavi, virumpi,
vāṉuṟa nivanta mēlnili maruṅkiṉ
1vēṉiṟ paḷḷi ēṟi, māṇiḻi,
teṉkaṭal muttum teṉmalic cantum
taṉkaṭaṉ iṟukkum taṉmiya ātaliṉ,
koṅki muṉṟil kuṅkuma 2vaḷākattu
miyaṟu ciṟappiṉ 3kiyuṟi ēnti
4atirā marapiṉ yāḻki vāṅki
matura kītam pāṭiṉaḷ.
inta matura kītam cempāli yeṉṉum paṇ eṉpar. itaṉi mutaṟkaṇ kaṇṭattāl pāṭi mayaṅki ataṉiyē yāḻiṉum pāṭiṉaḷ; ataṉālum, tāṉ kōvalaṉip pirinta tuyar vēṉil viruppāl mikuvatāl, āṟṟāḷāy, palavakip paṇkaḷiyum pāṭiṉaḷ. avaṟṟālum avaḷatu tuyar nīṅkavilli. piṉpu oruvāṟu tēṟi, kōvalaṉukkut tirumuka moṉṟeḻuta maṉattāl eṇṇiṉāḷ. uṭaṉē, tāḻip pūviṉatu veḷḷiyamaṭali ōli naṟukkākavum 5pittikiyiṉ kūṟiya arumpi eḻuttāṇiyākavum koṇṭu, cempañciṉ kuḻampil tōyttu utaṟi eḻutalāṉāḷ.
maṉṉuyi rellām makiḻtuṇi puṇarkkum
iṉṉiḷa vēṉil iḷavara cāḷaṉ
6antip pōtakattu 7arumpiṭart tōṉṟiya
tiṅkaṭ celvaṉum 8cevviya ṉallaṉ;
puṇarnta mākkaḷ poḻutuiṭip paṭuppiṉum
9taṇanta mākkaḷ tamtuṇi maṟappiṉum
naṟumpū 10vāḷiyiṉ nalluyir kōṭal
iṟumpū taṉṟuiḵtu aṟintī miṉeṉat
taṉ āṟṟāmikkuṟippiyum, tāṉ kaṟṟa nāṭakak kalivakikaḷuḷ kōvalaṉōṭutāṉ kūṟi iruntapōtu avaṉpāl nikaḻttik kāṭṭiya 1varivakikaḷiyum vāyāṟ collic colli,
2viḷiyā maḻaliyiṉ viritturi eḻutip
pacanta mēṉiyaḷ paṭaruṟu māliyiṉ
vacanta māliyi varukeṉak kūuyt
tūmalar māliyiṉ tuṇiporuḷ ellām
kōvalaṟku aḷittuk 3koṇarka īṅkueṉa,
avaḷ ataṉi eṭuttuc ceṉṟaṉaḷ. kōvalaṉ kūlavītiyil iruppak kaṇṭu, avaḷ at tirumukatti avaṉpāl koṭuttāḷ. avaṉ ataṉip paṭittu, vacantamāliyi nōkki, “naṅkāy, itaṉuṭ kūṟiyuḷḷa,
kātalil tōṉṟiya 4kaṇkūṭu variyum,
karuneṭuṅ kaṇṇi 5kāṇvarik kōlamum,
ceruvēl neṭuṅkaṇ 6cilatiyar kōlattu
orutaṉi vanta 7uḷvari yāṭalum,
puṟattuniṉ ṟāṭiya 8puṉpuṟa variyum,
kiḷarntuvē ṟākiya 9kiḷarvarik kōlamum,
tērntutērn turitta 10tērccivari yaṉṟiyum,
kaṇṭavark kuritta 1kāṭci variyum,
eṭuttavar tīrtta 2eṭuttukkōḷ variyum
āṭal makaḷē yātaliṉ, āyiḻi!
3pāṭu peṟṟaṉaap pintoṭi taṉakku” 
eṉa urittut taṉ vārāmiyum colli viṭṭāṉ. avaṉ maṟuttatu kaṇṭa vacantamāli ceyalaṟṟu, melivumuṟṟāḷ. piṉpu avaḷ vāṭiya uḷḷamuṭaṉ virinta mātavipāl ceṉṟu, kōvalaṉ kūṟiyatum, maṟuttatum viriyak kūṟi, avaṉi “aṉpillātavaṉ, koṭiyaṉ; iṉi, avaṉ vārāṉ” eṉap paḻitturittāḷ. atu kēṭkap poṟāta mātavi, nāṭakak kaṇiki yāyiṉum kaṟpum poṟpum ciṟakkappeṟṟavaḷātalāl, vacanta mālikkut “tōḻi, avarik koṭumi kūṟātē; aruṭ celvar; avar,
māli vārā rāyiṉum māṇiḻi,
kālikāṇ kuvam” eṉak kiyaṟu neñcamoṭu
pūmalar amaḷimicip poruntātu vatintaṉaḷ.
9. kaṉāttiṟam uritta kāti
mālati eṉṉumoru pārppaṉi taṉ māṟṟāḷ kuḻantikku oru nāḷ caṅku koṇṭu pāl ūṭṭa, ak kuḻanti pāl vikki iṟantatu. atu kaṇṭu añciṉa avaḷ, caṭṭeṉa ataṉi eṭuttuk koṇṭu amarar taruk kōṭṭam mutalākap palvakik kōṭṭaṅ kaṭkum ceṉṟu muṭivil cāttaṉār kōṭṭattil pāṭu kiṭantāḷ. atu kāli, iṭākiṉi eṉṉum pēy ak kuḻantiyi eṭuttu viḻuṅki oḻintatu. maṉam iraṅkiya cāttaṉār avaḷit tēṟṟip pōkac colli vaḻiyil tāṉē ak kuḻanti uruvāyk kiṭantār. avaḷ maṉat tuyar nīṅkiṟṟu. ak kuḻantiyāṉa cāttaṉār vaḷarntu kalvi nirampi, peṟṟōrkku iṟutik kaṭaṉ ceytu, tēvanti eṉṉum peṇṇi maṇantu eṭṭiyāṇṭu taṉ maṉiviyoṭu vāḻntu paḻiyapaṭiyē tēvavuruk koṇṭār. tēvanti aṉṟu mutalē ac cāttaṉār kōyili vaḻipaṭṭu varuvāḷāyiṉāḷ. ivaḷukku kaṇṇaki taṉ koḻunaṉip pirinta taṉāl tuṉpuṟuvatu terintatu. ataṉāl, avaḷōṭu naṭpuṟṟu avaṭkut tōḻiyāy iruntu vantāḷ. oru nāḷ tēvanti kaṇṇakiyiṉ mukam nōkki, “nī niṉ kaṇavaṉatu kūṭṭam peṟuka” eṉṟu vāḻttiṉāḷ. ataṉāl kaṇṇakikku uvakiyuṇṭāyiṟṟā yiṉum, “nī kūṟumāṟu yāṉ eṉ kaṇavaṉip peṟuvēṉ; āṉāl,
1kaṭukkumeṉ neñcam kaṉaviṉāl; eṉki
2piṭittaṉaṉ pōyōr perumpatiyuṭ paṭṭēm;
paṭṭa patiyil paṭāta toruvārtti
iṭṭaṉar ūrār, 3iṭutēḷiṭṭu eṉtaṉmēl,
kōvalaṟku uṟṟatuōr tīṅkueṉṟu; atukēṭṭuk
kāvalaṉ muṉṉaryāṉ kaṭṭurittēṉ; kāvalaṉōṭu
ūrkkuṟṟa tīṅkum oṉṟuuṇṭāl; 1uriyāṭēṉ;
2tīkkuṟṟam pōlum, ceṟitoṭīi! tīkkuṟṟam
uṟṟēṉōṭu uṟṟa 3uṟuvaṉōṭu yāṉuṟṟa
4naṟṟiṟam kēṭkiṉ nakiyākum”
eṉṟu tāṉ kaṇṭa kaṉāviṉ tiṟattik kaṭṭurittāḷ.
ataṉik kēṭṭa tēvanti,
5kittāyum alli; kaṇavaṟku orunōṉpu
poyttāḷ paḻampiṟappil; 6pōykkeṭuka; uyttuk
kaṭaloṭu kāviri ceṉṟalikkum 7muṉṟil
maṭalaviḻ neytalaṅ kāṉal 8taṭamuḷa;
cōmakuṇṭam cūriya kuṇṭam tuṟimūḻkik
kāmavēḷ kōṭṭam toḻutār, kaṇavaroṭu
tāmiṉ puṟuvar ulakattut tiyalār;
9pōkamcey pūmiyiṉum pōyp piṟappar; yām oru nāḷ āṭutum,
eṉṟu coṉṉāḷ. ataṟku, kaṇṇaki, kaṟpuṭi makaḷir tam kaṇavari aṉṟip piṟa teyvaṅkaḷik ki toḻutal ilar eṉṟa karuttāl, atu “pīṭaṉṟu” eṉa  maṟuttiruntāḷ. ivvāṟu irukkiyil, vāyilil irunta ēvalāṭṭi vantu, “kāvalaṉākiya kōvalaṉ vantu nam kaṭiyakattuḷḷāṉ” eṉṟāḷ. kaṇṇakiyum virintu ceṉṟu mukamalarntu varavēṟṟāḷ. avaḷōṭu iṉitirunta kōvalaṉ, kaṇṇakiyiṉ varuntiya niliyum vāṭiya mēṉiyum kaṇṭu peru varuttam koṇṭu, neñcu kaluḻntu,
1calampuṇar koḷkic 2calatiyo ṭāṭik
kulamtaru 3vāṉporuṭ kuṉṟam tolinta
4ilampāṭu nāṇut tarumeṉakku
eṉṟāṉ. mātavikkuk koṭukkum poruḷ kuṟintataṉāl ivvāṟu ivaṉ taḷarntu kūṟiṉāṉ eṉṟu koṇṭu, kaṇṇaki, “ēṉi aṇikalamellām oḻiyiṉum, yāṉ aṇiyātu vit tirukkum cilampukaḷ uḷa; avaṟṟik koṇṭu celka” eṉṟu parintu kūṟiṉāḷ. kōvalaṉ maṉam puṇpaṭṭu, “cēyiḻi, kēḷ: ic
“cilampu mutalākac ceṉṟa kalaṉōṭu
  5ulantaporuḷ 6īṭṭuta luṟṟēṉ; malarntacīr
  māṭa maturi yakattuc ceṉṟu”
eṉṟu colli, “poḻutu viṭitaṟkuḷ pōtaṟku niṉintēṉ; nīyum eṉṉōṭu eḻuka” eṉa moḻintāṉ.
kātali kaṇṭa kaṉavu karuneṭuṅkaṇ
mātavitaṉ colli vaṟitākka - 7mūti
viṉi kaṭik kūṭṭa 8viyamkoṇṭāṉ kaṅkul
kaṉi 9cuṭar kālcīyā muṉ.
10. nāṭukāṇ kāti
1vāṉkaṇ viḻiyā vikaṟi yāmattu
2mīṉtikaḻ vicumpiṉ veṇmati nīṅkak
kāriruḷ niṉṟa 3kaṭināṭ kaṅkul
ūḻviṉi 4kaṭii uḷḷam 5turappak
kōvalaṉ, kaṇṇaki nallāḷuṭaṉ pukār nakarattiṉ tōraṇa vāyilik kaṭantu, maṇivaṇṇaṉ kōyili aṭintu avaṉi valam ceytu vaṇaṅki, putta tarumaṅkaḷi antara cārikaḷ maṟiya iruntu ōtutaṟkāka intiraṉ nirumitta intira vikāram ēḻum poruntiya putta cittiyatti aṭintāṉ. ataṉpiṉ arukaṉ kōyilil uḷḷa perumaṉṟattil niṉṟa acōka marattiṉ niḻalil 6ulaka nōṉpikaḷ iṭṭa cilātalattik kaṇṭu toḻutu valam ceytu koṇṭu, ūr vāyilik kaṭantu, iruvarum,
7kaliyi lāḷaṉ kāmar vēṉiloṭu
malaya mārutam 8maṉṉavaṟku iṟukkum
paṉmalar aṭukkiya naṉmarap pantar
9ilavan tikiyiṉ eyiṟpuṟam pōkik
kāvirikkuc cellum peru vaḻiyit toṭarntu ceṉṟu, ataṉ kari vāyilic cērntaṉar; piṉṉar, kāviriyiṉ vaṭakari vaḻiyāka mēṟkut tikkunōkki āṅkē ceṟintirunta malarp poḻilkaḷiṉūṭē oru kāvatam naṭantu kavuntip paḷḷiyik kaṇṭaṭintaṉar. ap paḷḷiyiṟciṟitupōtu iruppa, kaṇṇaki, kōvalaṉi nōkki, puṉmuṟuvaluṭaṉ, “maturi mūtūr yātu?” eṉa viṉava, avaṉ, “nallāy! atu āṟiṅkāta aḷavil (muppatu kāta tūrattil) uḷatu; cēyttaṉṟu, naṇittē” eṉṟāṉ. avaḷ nakittu makiḻa, iruvarum ap paḷḷiyiṉkaṇ vāḻnta kavunti aiyiyik kaṇṭu aṭi toḻutaṉar. avarkaḷik kaṇṭa kavunti, makiḻnta uḷḷattaḷāy,
uruvum kulaṉum uyarpē roḻukkamum
1perumakaṉ tirumoḻi piṟaḻā nōṉpum
uṭiyīr! eṉṉō, 2uṟuka ṇāḷariṉ
3kaṭikaḻintu iṅṅaṉam karutiya vāṟu?
eṉṟu kēṭṭaṉaḷ. kōvalaṉ, “uṟutavattīr, uriyāṭṭu oṉṟum illi. maturi mūtūr ceṉṟu poruḷ īṭṭum vēṭki yuṭiyēṉ” eṉṟāṉ. “atu karuttāyiṉ, nīvir neṟiyiṉ arumi aṟintilīr; atu kāṭum nāṭum iṭiyiṭṭup parukkikaḷ niṟintirukkum; ivaḷ ataṉik kaṭantu pōtaṟku vēṇṭum taṉmiyuṭiyavaḷallaḷ; iṉi ūḻ eṉ ceytu muṭikkumō? ataṉi aṟivār yār? aṉṟiyum uyarkuṭip piṟanta nī ivaḷi oruṅku koṇṭu celvatu ēlātu, ātalāl celvati oḻika eṉiṉ, nīvir oḻikiṉṟilīr” eṉṟu mutaṟkaṇ moḻintu, piṉpu,
“4maṟavuri nītta mācaṟu kēḷviyar
aṟavuri kēṭṭuāṅku 5aṟivaṉi ēttat
teṉtamiḻ naṉṉāṭṭut tītutīr maturikku
6oṉṟiya uḷḷa muṭiyēṉ; ākaliṉ,
1pōtuval yāṉum; pōtumiṉ” eṉṟa
kāvunti aiyiyik kitoḻutu ētti
“aṭikaḷ nīrē aruḷuti rāyiṉ, it
2toṭivaḷit tōḷi tuyartīrt tēṉ” 
eṉak kōvalaṉ uvaki mikuntu kūṟalum, kavunti, “kōvala, nī cellutaṟku mēṟkoṇṭa vaḻiyiṉkaṇ tuṉpam taruvaṉa palavum uṇṭu; kēḷ” eṉṟu vaḻiyiṉ arumikaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟalāṉāḷ.
3veyilniṟam poṟāa melliyaṟ koṇṭu
payilpūn taṇṭalip 4paṭarkuva meṉiṉē,

caṇpakam niṟitta 6tātucōr poṅkar
poyyaṟip paṭuttup 7pōṟṟā mākkaṭkuk

nīrnāy kuviya 1neṭumpuṟa vāḷi
2malaṅkumiḷir 3ceṟuvil vilaṅkap pāyiṉ
kalaṅkalum uṇṭuik kāriki; āṅkaṇ,
karumpil toṭutta peruntēṉ citintu
curumpucūḻ poykit tūnīr kalakkum;

6kuṟunar iṭṭa kuvaḷiyam pōtoṭu

eṟinīr aṭikari iyakkam taṉṉil

vaḻiyil, kāviriyiṉ putunīrp perukku matakukaḷil muṭuki yēkum ōciyum, vayalkaḷil kampuṭkōḻiyum, nāriyum piṟavumākiya puḷḷiṉaṅkaḷiṉ oliyum,
1karuṅki viṉiñarum kaḷamarum kūṭi

pāruṭip paṉarpōl 4paḻicciṉar kitoḻa
ērōṭu niṉṟōr 5ērmaṅ kalamum,
6peruñceyn nelliṉ 7mukavip pāṭṭum,

9maṇkaṉi muḻaviṉ makiḻici yōtiyum,
kēṭṭavaṇṇam ceṉṟa im mūvarum cila ūrkaḷik kaṭantu ceṉṟaṉar. aṅkē, maṟiyōr irukkiyum,
parappunīrk kāvirip pāvitaṉ putalvar
irappōr cuṟṟamum 10purappōr koṟṟamum
uḻaviṭi viḷippōr paḻaviṟal ūrkaḷum
kaṇṭu makiḻntaṉar. āyiṉum, ivvāṟṟāl ivarkaḷ oru kāvatamē cella muṭintatu. muṭivil āṟṟiṭikkuṟi oṉṟu ivarkaḷ muṉ tōṉṟiṟṟu. ataṉarukē vāṉavar uṟiyum poḻiloṉṟu iruntatu. ataṉkaṇ, ulaka nōṉpikaḷ iṭṭa cilātalam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭṭatu. ataṉ mēl tarumam urikkum cāraṇar tōṉṟa, avar tiruvaṭikaḷil im mūvarum vīḻntu vaṇaṅkiṉar. ivaruḷ kōvalaṉum kaṇṇakiyum,
vanta kāraṇam vayaṅkiya koḷkic
1cinti viḷakkil terintō ṉāyiṉum
ārvamum 2teṟṟamum 3akala nīkkiya
vīraṉākaliṉ 4viḻumam koḷḷāṉ;
kaḻiperuñ ciṟappiṉ kavunti! kāṇāy.
oḻikeṉa oḻiyātu ūṭṭum valviṉi,
5iṭṭa vittiṉ etirntuvan teyti
oṭṭuṅ kāli oḻikkavum oṇṇā;

cāraṇar vāymoḻi kēṭṭut tavamutal
kāvunti, kitaṉ talimēṟ koṇṭu,
oru 7mūṉ ṟavittōṉ ōtiya ñāṉat
tirumoḻik kallatu eṉ ceviyakam tiṟavā;

nāma mallatu navilātu eṉnā;
8aivari veṉṟōṉ aṭiyiṇi yallatu
9kivarik kāṇiṉum kāṇā eṉkaṇ;
aruḷ aṟam pūṇṭōṉ tirumeyk kallatueṉ
1poruḷil yākki pūmiyiṟ poruntātu;
arukar aṟavaṉ aṟivōṟ kallatueṉ
irukiyum kūṭi oruvaḻik kuviyā;
2malarmici naṭantōṉ malaraṭi allatueṉ
talimici yucci tāṉ 3aṇip poṟāatu;

4maṟutara vōtieṉ maṉampuṭi peyarātu,
eṉṟu kavuntiyaṭikaḷ paravik kūṟavē, cāraṇar nilattiliruntu oru muḻa vuyaram antarattē niṉṟu, “pavan tarupācam kavunti keṭuka” eṉṟu urittuviṭṭu vāṉavītiyiṟ paṭarntaṉar.
piṉṉar, kavuntiyaṭikaḷoṭu kōvalaṉum kaṇṇakiyum ōṭamēṟi āṟṟik kaṭantu kāviriyiṉ teṉkariyi aṭintu, oru pūm poḻilil iruntaṉar. aṅkē, vampap parattiyorutti yuṭaṉ vaṟumoḻiyāḷaṉ oruvaṉ vantu, kōvalaṉiyum kaṇṇaki yiyum kaṇṭu viyantu,
kāmaṉum tēviyum pōlum īṅkuivar
ār? eṉak kēṭṭu 5īṅku aṟikuvam eṉṟē
6nōṟṟuṇal yākki nocitavat tīr! uṭaṉ

makkaḷ; kāṇīr, māṉiṭa yākkiyar;

kaṭavatum uṇṭō? kaṟṟaṟin tīreṉat
1tīmoḻi kēṭṭuc ceviyakam putittuk

2. maturik kāṇṭam
11. kāṭukāṇ kāti
ivvāṟu uṟiyūri yaṭinta kōvalaṉum kaṇṇakiyum, kavuntiyaṭikaḷum, aṅkuḷḷa,
1tiṅkaḷmūṉ ṟaṭukkiya tirumuk kuṭikkīḻc
ceṅkatir ñāyiṟṟut 2tikaḻoḷi ciṟantu,
3kōtitāḻ piṇṭik koḻuniḻal irunta
4ātiyil tōṟṟattu 5aṟivaṉi vaṇaṅki,
avaṉatu kōyilil irunta camaṇa muṉivarkaḷik kaṇṭaṉar. avarkaṭku āṟṟiṭik kuṟiyil cāvakar iṭṭa cilātalattiṉ mēl iruntukoṇṭu, cāraṇar coṉṉa aṟavurikaḷik kavuntiyaṭikaḷ urittaṉar. aṉṟu pakaṟpōtu kaḻiyavē, im mūvarum ap paḷḷiyilēyē iravup poḻutiyum kaḻittaṉar. maṟunāṭ kāliyil veyyavaṉ kīḻt ticiyil viḷaṅkit tōṉṟa, im mūvarum cellat toṭaṅki, neṭitu ceṉṟaṉar. vaḻiyil, iḷamarak kāvoṉṟu avar kaṇṇetirē tōṉṟiṟṟu. av viṭatti aṭintu ivarkaḷ irukkumpōtu, maṟiyavaṉ oruvaṉ,

eṉpaḷ. avaṭku avaḷ kēṭṭa muṟiyē poruḷ nalamum aṟaviṉic ciṟappum, vīṭupēṟṟiyalpum virittuk kūṟuvīrāyiṉ, pilavaḻikku vēṇṭum katavit tiṟantu vaḻi kāṭṭuvāḷ. av vaḻiyē celliṉ, cēymikkaṇ iraṭṭik katavu oṉṟu uṅkaṭkut tōṉṟum. ataṉi aṭiyiṉ, āṅkē orutti tōṉṟi,
1iṟutiyil iṉpam eṉakkuīṅku urittāl,
peṟutir pōlumnīr pēṇiya poruḷ, eṉum;
uriyī rāyiṉum 2uṟukaṇ ceyyēṉ;
3neṭuvaḻip puṟattu nīkkuvaṉ numeṉum.
orukāl yāvarēṉum avaḷ kēṭpataṉi urittāl avar kaḷi muṉpu kūṟiya puṇṇiya caravaṇam mutaliya mūṉṟaṉiyum kāṭṭi virumpiyatu ceyka eṉṟu colli nīṅkuvaḷ; nīvirum avaḷāl am mūṉṟum kāṭṭappaṭṭu, ainteḻuttum eṭṭeḻuttu mākiya maṟimoḻikaḷuḷ virumpiya toṉṟi niṉittu vāyāl moḻintu, avaṟṟuṭ paṭivīrāyiṉ, malimici irukkum periyōṉ tiruvaṭiyip poruntuvīrkaḷ.
4uḷḷam poruntuvī rāyiṉ, maṟṟuavaṉ,
5puḷḷaṇi nīḷkoṭi puṇarnili tōṉṟum;

7māṇpuṭi marapiṉ maturikku ēkumiṉ.
eṉṟu colli, ituvē ap pilavaḻiyiṉ kāṭci eṉṟu coṉṉāṉ. itaṟku mēlum collaluṟṟa ammaṟiyavaṉ, “itaṉiyum nīvir virumpīrāyiṉ iṭiyē yuḷḷa vaḻiyē ceṉmiṉ; atuvē cevviya neṟi. vaḻiyil nāṭukaḷum ūrkaḷum palavuṇṭu; ariya vaḻikaḷ cilavum kāṇappaṭum. avvaruvaḻiyil, mikka tuyar ceyyum teyvamum oṉṟu uṇṭu. atu vaḻippōvārkku veḷippaṭiyāka yātoru tīṅkum ceyyātu. nayantōṉṟa naṭittup pōkiṉṟavarkaḷip pōkā vaṇṇam ceytuviṭum; ataṉit tappic celliṉ, maturip patiyi eḷitil iṉitu aṭivīrkaḷ. yāṉum, tirumāli vaḻipaṭac celkiṉṟēṉ,” eṉṟu collic celavupeṟac camintāṉ. avaṉ kūṟiyavaṟṟi itukāṟum mikka amitiyuṭaṉ kēṭṭuk koṇṭē vanta kavuntiyaṭikaḷ,
1nalampuri koḷki nāṉmaṟi yāḷa,

eṉṟu moḻintaṉar; maṟiyavaṉum pōyc cērntāṉ. piṉṉar mūvarum maturikkuriya vaḻiyē naṭakkaluṟṟaṉar; aṉṟiya pōtu vēṟōr ariya ūriṉkaṇ taṅkiyiruntu, aṭuttanāṭ kāliyil celvārāyiṉar. pōm pōtu veyil mikkatu; ēṉi iruvarkkum nīr vēṭki mikkatu. kiḷivaḻi oṉṟik kōvalaṉ piṉpaṟṟic ceṉṟu oru poykik kariyi aṭintāṉ. aṅkē oru teyvam, mātaviyiṉ tōḻiyāṉa vacantamālipōl urukkoṇṭu pōntu, mātaviyiṉ uṭkōḷik kaṇṭu kūṟuvatu pōla, nayamoḻikaḷi mutaṟkaṇ colli,
“6mēlō rāyiṉum 7nūlō rāyiṉum
8pālvaki terinta pakutiyō rāyiṉum

eṉa mātavi maṉam aḻintu vāḻkkiyil veṟuppaṭintu eṉṉiyum vilakkiviṭṭāḷ; yāṉum nīvir maturik kēkuvatu kēḷviyuṟṟu, vāṇikac cāttōṭu taṉiyē vantu tuyaruḻantēṉ; eṉakku yātu paṇikkiṉṟāy? eṉṟu kēṭṭatu. “itu orukāl am maṟiyōṉ coṉṉa mayakkum teyvamō” eṉak kōvalaṉ aiyuṟṟut taṉ nāviṉāl oru maṟimoḻiyic collavē, teyvam taṉ uṇmi vaṭivu kāṭṭi,
4vaṉacā riṇiyāṉ; mayakkam ceytēṉ;

12. vēṭṭuva vari
kavuntiyaṭikaḷum kōvalaṉum kaṇṇakiyumākiya mūvarum aiyik kōṭṭattil oru puṟattē taṅkiyiruntaṉar. akkālam vēṉiṟkālam. veyilavaṉatu veppam tāṅkutaṟku aritāyiruntatu. vaḻippaṟi ceyyuṅ kaḷvar kūṭṭamum vaḻiccelvōr illāmi yāl tattam cēriyil maṭintu kiṭantatu. kaḷavē purintu vāḻum vēṭṭuvar, tamakkuk kaḷavu vāykkāmiyāl vaṟumi mikku varuntuvārāyiṉar. atupōtu,
1vaḻaṅkuvil taṭakki 2maṟakkuṭit tāyattup
3paḻaṅkaṭaṉ uṟṟa 4muḻaṅkuvāyc cāliṉi

1iṭumuḷ vēli eyiṉarkūṭṭu uṇṇum
naṭuvūr maṉṟattu 2aṭipeyarttu āṭi,
“eyiṉarkaḷē, āṟalittal, cūṟiyāṭutal, ākōṭal mutaliya uṅkaṭku muṟimiyāṉa toḻilkaḷ ippōtu vāykkāmi yāl nīvir vaṟumiyuṟṟīrkaḷ. uṅkaḷ cilikku veṟṟi tanta kaliyūr celvikku nīvir uyirppali tārā toḻintīrkaḷ. uṅkaḷuṭiya 3kaḷavēr vāḻkki muṉ pōla vaḷam peṟa vēṇṭumāyiṉ, ippōtē avaṭkuriya kaṭaṉik koṭumiṉ” eṉṟu coṉṉāḷ. atu kēṭṭa eyiṉar ataṟku uṭampaṭṭu, ac cāliṉiyiṉ kūntalic caṭiyākap piṉṉi, veḷḷiya kayiṟṟāl kaṭṭi, paṉṟiyiṉ kaṭivāyp pallip piṟipōlac cūṭṭi, kaḻuttiṟkup pulippal kōtta tāliyaṇintu, pulittōli mēkaliyāka uṭuttu, valiya villoṉṟi avaḷ kiyil koṭuttu, muṟukkuṇṭa kompiyuṭiya kalimāṉmītu  ēṟṟi, koṭṭum, paṟiyum, kompum, kuḻalum muḻaṅki, muṉ cella, avar naṭuvē, ac cāliṉi, kalimāṉmītu ēṟivara,
4vaṇṇamum cuṇṇamum taṇnaṟum cāntamum

eyiṉar makaḷirāṉa eyiṟṟiyar ēntikkoṇṭu piṉṉē vara, aiyikkōṭṭam aṭintaṉar. aṅkēyuḷḷa pali pīṭikiyiṉ muṉ avaḷik koṇarntu niṟuttit tām koṇṭu pōnta poruḷkaḷi ap pīṭikiyil vittu eyiṉar aṉivarum aiyiyik kitoḻutu ēttiṉar. appōtu, kaliyēṟi vanta cāliṉi, teyva maruḷkoṇṭu orupuṟattē irunta kaṇṇakiyik kaṇṭu, aṅku niṉṟa eyiṉarkkuk kāṭṭi,

13. puṟañcēri iṟutta kāti
koṟṟavik kōlam pūṇṭu vēṭṭuva makaḷ kūttāṭip pala pāṭṭup pāṭi, vēṭṭuvar cūḻa ak koṟṟaviyip paravi nīṅkiya piṉpu, kōvalaṉ kavuntiyaṭikaḷi vaṇaṅki, “ik kaṇṇaki, kaṭu veyil niṉṟa iv vēṉil veppattip poṟāḷ; ivaḷuṭiya melliya aṭikaḷ ittakiya vaḻikaḷi naṭantaṟiyātavi.
                           veṅkāṉattuk
1kōḷval uḷiyamum koṭumpuṟṟu akaḻā;
2vāḷvari vēṅkiyum māṉkaṇam maṟalā;
aravum 3cūrum iritēr mutaliyum
4urumum cārntavarkku uṟukaṇ ceyyā;
ceṅkōl teṉṉavar kākkum nāṭueṉa
5eṅkaṇum pōkiya iciyō peritē;
ātalāl, pakaṟpōtiṟ celvatilum iravuppōtiṟ celvatu naṉṟu” eṉṟu irantu kūṟiṉaṉ. kavuntiyaṭikaḷum ataṟku icintaṉar. pakaṟpōtu mellak kaḻivatāyiṟṟu,
koṭuṅkōl vēntaṉ kuṭikaḷ pōlap
1paṭuṅkatir amayam pārttirun tōrkkup
2paṉmīṉ tāṉiyoṭu pālkatir parappit

mūvarum maturikkēkum vaḻiyippaṟṟic cellat toṭaṅkiṉar. vaḻiyil, kūkikaḷ kuḻaṟiṉa; koṭuvarip pulikaḷ kuḻumit tirintaṉa. ‘avaṟṟiṟku añcavēṇṭā’ eṉat takuvaṉa kūṟik kaṇṇakiyit teruṭṭi, kōvalaṉ, kavuntiyaṭikaḷ aruḷiya aṟavurikaḷik kēṭṭukkoṇṭē iṉitu ceṉṟaṉaṉ. viṭiyaṟpōtil, mūvarum pārppaṉar uṟiyum ōr ūriṉ puṟattē irunta takka iṭatti aṭintu taṅkiṉar. atu nalla cēmamuḷḷatōr iṭamātal kaṇṭu teḷinta kōvalaṉ, avarkaḷi avviṭattē irutti, tāṉmaṭṭil kālikkaṭaṉ kaḻippatu kuṟittu, avar irunta iṭattic cūḻavirunta muḷ vēliyi nīkkikkoṇṭu nīr nili yoṉṟi nāṭic ceṉṟāṉ. avaṉ ceṉṟaviṭattē, kavucikaṉ eṉṉumoru pārppāṉ kōvalaṉik kaṇṭāṉ. avaṉ kōvalaṉik kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattē naṉku aṟintavaṉ; mātaviyāl kōvalaṉ irukki nāṭi aṟintuvarum poruṭṭuc celuttappaṭṭavaṉ. avaṉ kōvalaṉik kaṇṭatum, avaṉatu mēṉi vēṟupāṭṭāl mutaṟkaṇ mayaṅki, uṇmi aṟital vēṇṭi, aṅkēyirunta mātavik koṭiyi nōkki, tāṉ colvatu akkōvalaṉ ceviyiṟ paṭumāṟu,
kōvalaṉ piriyak koṭuntuyar eytiya
1māmalar neṭuṅkaṇ mātavi pōṉṟuiv
2aruntiṟal vēṉiṟku alarkaḷintu uṭaṉē
varuntiṉi pōlum, nī, mātavi!
eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ. atu kēṭṭu viyappuk koṇṭa kōvalaṉ, “nī yātu kūṟiṉi?” eṉṟaṉaṉ. eṉavē, kōcikaṉ, kōvalaṉi uṇmik kōvalaṉē eṉat teḷintu, kōvalaṉ pirintu vantapiṉ kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattu nikaḻntatu kūṟaluṟṟu, kōvalaṉatu peṟṟōr tuyarnili, cuṟṟattavar nili, ūravarnili, mātaviyiṉ tuyarnili ākiya ivaṟṟik kūṟi, “vayanta māliyāl niṉ pirivu aṟintatum, mātavi 3cēkkip paḷḷiyil mayaṅki vīḻntāḷ. ataṉik kēḷvi yuṟṟa yāṉ viriyac ceṉṟu avaḷ tiruvaṭi toḻutēṉ; ‘kaṇmaṇiyaṉiyāṟkuk kāṭṭuka’ eṉṟu muṭaṅkal oṉṟu eḻuti eṉ kiyil tantu niṉṉiṭam cērkka eṉak koṭut tāḷ” eṉṟu colli, am muṭaṅkaliyum kōvalaṉ kiyil nīṭṭiṉaṉ. ataṉpāl mātavi iṭṭirunta kūntaṟ poṟiyāl avaḷatu maṉattūymiyi uṇarntu, kōvalaṉ, ataṉik ki nekiḻātu virittuk kāṇaluṟṟāṉ. ataṉkaṇ,

14. ūrkāṇ kāti
kavuntiyaṭikaḷuṭaṉ kōvalaṉum kaṇṇakiyum maturi mūtūriṉ puṟañciṟip poḻili yaṭutta mātavar uṟiyum cēriyi aṭinta aḷavil viṭiyal varuvatāyiṟṟu. kūmpi irunta tāmarikaḷ poti aviḻat toṭaṅkiṉa; cōliyilum, kaḻaṉiyilum, poykiyilum puḷḷiṉam eḻuntu ārkkat toṭaṅkiṉa; ceññāyiṟu ceṅkatirip parappik kīḻit ticiyil eḻat toṭaṅkiṉaṉ. maturiyil uḷḷa
1nutalviḻi nāṭṭattu iṟiyōṉ kōyilum,
2uvaṇac cēval uyarttōṉ niyamamum,
3mēḻi valaṉuyartta veḷḷi nakaramum,
kōḻic cēval 4koṭiyōṉ kōṭṭamum,
aṟattuṟi viḷaṅkiya aṟavōr paḷḷiyum,
5maṟattuṟi viḷaṅkiya maṉṉavaṉ kōyilum,
vālveṇ caṅkoṭu vakipeṟṟu ōṅkiya
kāli muracam.
kaṉikural eṭuttu iyampiṉa. kōvalaṉ kavuntiyaṭikaḷit toḻutu, “tavattīr, illaṟa neṟiyiliruntu nīṅkiyōr koḷḷum taṉmiyuṭiyēṉāy, muṉṉaṟiyāta nāṭṭiṟkup pala vaḻikaḷi naṭantu kaṭantu, im melliyal nallāḷit tuṉpuṟuttic ciṟumi yuṟṟēṉ. in nakarkkaṇ pukuntu iṅkuḷḷa vaṇikarkku eṉ nilimiyiṉi uṇartti, yāṉ mīṇṭu varuṅkālum, ivaḷ taṅkaḷ tiruvaṭik kāppiṉaḷātalvēṇṭum, itaṉāl taṅkaṭku ētēṉum ēta muṇṭō?” eṉa vēṇṭiṉaṉ. atu kēṭṭa kavuntiyaṭikaḷ, “nī muṟpiṟappil peritum nalviṉiyē ceytu, ciṟitu tīviṉi ceytaṉi; ataṉāl it tuyar uḻantāy. aṉṟiyum itaṉik kēḷ,”
1maṟattuṟi nīṅkumiṉ valviṉi ūṭṭumeṉṟu
2aṟattuṟi mākkaḷ tiṟattiṉ cāṟṟi,
3nākkaṭip pāka vāyppaṟi aṟiyiṉum
4yāppaṟi mākkaḷ iyalpil koḷḷār;
tītuṭi vevviṉi 5urutta kālip
6pētimi kantāp perumpētu uṟuvar;
7oyyā viṉippayaṉ uṇṇum kālik
8kiyāṟu koḷḷār kaṟṟaṟi mākkaḷ;
pirital tuṉpamum, puṇartal tuṉpamum,
9uruvi lāḷaṉ oṟukkum tuṉpamum,
purikuḻal mātarp puṇarntōrk kallatu
10orutaṉi vāḻkki uravōrkku illi;
peṇṭirum uṇṭiyum iṉpameṉṟu ulakil

3ēmam cārā iṭumpi eytiṉar
iṉṟē yallāl 4iṟantōr palarāl,
5toṉṟu paṭa varūum toṉmittu,
eṉṟu viriyak kūṟi, ivaṟṟiṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka, kāṭṭil taṉ kātaliyi iḻantu varuntiya irāmaṉ kati, cūtāṭi nāṭiḻantu uṭaviyil naṭuviyāmattē taṉ kātaliyik kiviṭṭakaṉṟa naḷaṉ kati ākiya ivaṟṟi eṭuttōti, in nikaḻccikaḷuḷ, naḷaṉ, taṉ kātaliyik kiviṭṭu nīṅkiyataṟku aṭippaṭiyāka iruppatu,
valviṉi yaṉṟō! maṭantitaṉ piḻieṉac

peruṅki yāṉi iṉaniri peyarum

kaṇṭu perumakiḻcci eyti, mīṇṭu, kavuntiyaṭikaḷ irunta iṭattiṟku vantu cērntāṉ.
15. aṭikkalak kāti
maturi mūtūriṉ pala nalaṅkaḷiyum kaṇṭu pōnta kōvalaṉ, kavuntiyaṭikaḷi nōkki, an nakariṉ ciṟappiyum, ataṉkaṇ iruntu āṭci puriyum pāṇṭi vēntariṉ kōliṉ cemmiyum, kuṭiyiṉ taṇmiyum, vēliṉ koṟṟamum viḷaṅkak kūṟiṉaṉ. appōtu māṭalaṉ eṉṉum maṟiyavaṉ oruvaṉ aṅkē vantiruntāṉ. avaṉ kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattukkarukiluḷḷa talicceṅkāṉattic cērntaṉaṉ. avaṉ potiyil malikkuc ceṉṟu ataṉi valaṅkoṇṭu, kumariyam peruntuṟikkuc ceṉṟu nīrāṭi vantukoṇṭiruntāṉ. vaḻiyil naṭantu pōnta varuttam nīṅka, aṟavōr vikum ip pūmpoḻiliṭattē taṅkiyiruntāṉ. avaṉik kōvalaṉ kaṇṭatum, avaṉaṭiyil vīḻntu vaṇaṅkiṉaṉ. māṭalaṉ, kōvalaṉi vāḻtti, “kōvala, niṉakku mātavi payanta makaḷukku maṇimēkali eṉṟu peyariṭṭa kāli, nī perum poruḷ vaḻaṅkiṉi; atupōtu aṅku vanta peru mutu vētiyaṉi matam paṭṭa yāṉiyoṉṟu paṟṟik koḷḷak kaṇṭu, ataṉi aṭakki, avaṉi eyir kāttāy. pārppaṉi orutti aṟiyātu ceyta piḻikkāka avaḷik kiviṭṭu avaḷ kaṇavaṉ cella, avaṉip piṉ toṭarntu varuntiya avaḷ poruṭṭup perum poruḷ nalki, avaḷ taṉ kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ aṟam pala purintu iṉitu vāḻac ceytāy. pattiṉi oruttiyiṉ poruṭṭut tīṅku ceyta oruvaṉi aṟi kari poyttavari aṟintuṇṇum pūtam paṟṟik koḷḷa, avaṉatu tāy atu kaṇṭu mikka tuyar eytuvati nī kaṇṭu avaṉ poruṭṭu ap pūtattiṉpāṟ ceṉṟu, “eṉ uyirik koṇṭu ivaṉ uyirit taruka” eṉak kēṭṭaṉi; niṉakku, atu,
1narakaṉ uyirkku naluyir koṇṭu
parakati iḻakkum paṇpu 2īṅku illi;
eṉṟu maṟuttu avaṉik koṉṟuviṭalum, nī avaṉ tāykkum avaṉuṭiya cuṟṟattōrkkum mikka poruḷ nalkik kāttu ōmpiṉi. ivvāṟu palariyum
pallāṇṭu puranta 1illōr cemmal!
immic ceytaṉa yāṉaṟi nalviṉi;
2ummip payaṉkol! orutaṉi uḻantuit
3tiruttaku māmaṇik koḻuntuṭaṉ pōntatu,
eṉṟu moḻintāṉ. avaṉukkuk kōvalaṉ, tāṉ kaṇṭa toru kaṉavik kūṟaluṟṟu,

kāval vēntaṉ kaṭinakar taṉṉil,
nāṟu 5aiṅ kūntal naṭuṅkutuyar eytak
6kūṟikōṭ paṭṭuk 7kōṭṭumā ūravum,
aṇittaku purikuḻal āyiḻi taṉṉoṭum
8piṇippuaṟut tōrtam peṟṟi eytavum,
māmalar vāḷi vaṟunilattu eṟintu
kāmak kaṭavuḷ kiyaṟṟu ēṅka,
aṇitikaḻ 9pōti aṟavōṉ taṉmuṉ
maṇimē kaliyi mātavi aḷippavum,
naṉavu pōla 1naḷḷiruḷ yāmattuk
kaṉavu kaṇṭēṉ,
eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ. atu kēṭṭa māṭalaṉum kavuntiyaṭikaḷum, “kōvala, ivviṭattē tuṟavu pūṇṭōrallatu piṟar iruttalākātu; nīvir nakarkkuṭ pukuntu, num peṟṟōr 2peyar viḷakkam kūṟiṉ, aṅkuḷḷa vaṇikar nummit tam aka nakarkkaṇṇē iruttuvar; katir ceṉṟu maṟivataṉmuṉ celka” eṉṟaṉar. ak kālattē, puṟañciṟip paḷḷiyiluḷḷa 3iyakkikkup pāṟcōṟu paṭittu vaḻi paṭṭuvanta mātari eṉṉum iṭicci avvaḻiyē vantāḷ. avaḷ kavuntiyaṭikaḷik kaṇṭatum aṭivaṇaṅki niṟpa, avvaṭikaḷ,
ākāttu ōmpi āppayaṉ aḷikkum
kōvalar vāḻkki ōr 4koṭumpāṭu illi;
tītilaḷ; 5mutumakaḷ; 6cevviyaḷ; aḷiyaḷ;

ētam iṉṟu,
eṉa eṇṇi, am mātariyi nōkki,
mātari, kēḷ: im maṭantitaṉ kaṇavaṉ
tātiyik kēṭkiṉ, 7taṉkula vāṇar
arumporuḷ peṟunariṉ viruntetir koṇṭu
karuntaṭaṅ kaṇṇiyoṭu 8kaṭimaṉip paṭuttuvar.
1uṭip peruñ celvar maṉippukum aḷavum
2iṭikkula maṭantikku aṭikkalam tantēṉ.

tēmeṉ kūntal cilmalar peytu
3tūmaṭi uṭīit 4tollōr ciṟappiṉ

tāyum nīyē ākit tāṅku,

5kaṟpuk kaṭampūṇṭa itteyvam allatu

“6vāṉam poyyātu, vaḷampiḻippu aṟiyātu,
nīḷnila vēntar koṟṟam citiyātu,

attaku naluri aṟiyā yō, nī,
eṉaccolli, tāṉ collum itaṉik kaṭippiṭittāl am mātarikku varakkaṭavatoru payaṉ kūṟuvāḷāy,
tavattōr aṭikkalam tāṉciṟi tāyiṉum
mikappē riṉpam tarum; atu kēḷāy,

1miḷiyum, kiṭaṅkum, 2vaḷiviṟ poṟiyum,
3karuviral ūkamum, kalumiḻ kavaṇum.

vāyil kaḻintu taṉ vaḷamaṉiyi aṭintāḷ.
16. kolikkaḷak kāti
kaṇṇakiyi aṭikkalamākap peṟṟa mātari, avaḷik kōvalaṉuṭaṉ taṉ cērikkuk koṇṭuceṉṟu, taṉitta toru kāval naṉku aminta kuṭiyirukki nalki, ataṉ kaṇ iruntuvara viṭuttāḷ. kaṇṇakiyum aṅkē taṉakkut tuṇiyāka vanta iṭiyar makaḷiruṭaṉ nīrāṭi, nalluṭi uṭuttu, aḻakiya aṇi pūṇṭu viḷaṅkiṉaḷ. mātarikku aiyi eṉṟoru makaḷ uḷḷāḷ. am makaḷi mātari kaṇṇakikku aṭittoḻil ceyyap paṇikka, kaṇṇakiyum avaḷit taṉṉuṭaṉ irukkaveṉa vittukkoṇ ṭaṉaḷ. kōvalaṉum kaṇṇakiyum cāvaka nōṉpikaḷātalāl, iravu variṉ uṇavu koḷḷār eṉṟu koṇṭu, uriya kālattē aṭicil ākkuvataṟku, mātariyiṉ kuṟippuppaṭiyē, aṭṭilukku vēṇṭum kalaṅkaḷum piṟavum aiyi koṇarntu utaviṉaḷ. avaṟṟōṭu amiyātu, uṇaviṉporuṭṭu,
1kōḷip pākal koḻuṅkaṉit tiraḷkāy
2vāḷvarik koṭuṅkāy, mātuḷam pacuṅkāy,
māviṉ kaṉiyoṭu vāḻit tīṅkaṉi
3cāli yarici tampāl payaṉoṭu
kōlvaḷi mātē! koḷkeṉak koṭuppa,
kaṇṇakiyum makiḻcciyuṭaṉ ēṟṟu, taṉ kaṇavaṉukku iṉiya uṇavic camippāḷāyiṉaḷ. kāykaṟikaḷip pākam paṇṇu miṭattu avaḷatu mel viralkaḷ civantaṉa; pukiyāl tirumukam viyarttatu; cevviya kaṇkaḷum civantaṉa. iv vaṇṇam uṇavu camittāṉapiṉ, kōvalaṉi uṇṭaṟkaḻippa, avaṉ pōntu āṅkē makaḷirāl toṭukkap peṟṟa paṉantaṭukku oṉṟaṉi virippākakkoṇṭu ataṉ mēl amarntaṉaṉ. kaṇṇaki,
kaṭimalar aṅkiyil kātalaṉ aṭinīr
1cuṭumaṇ maṇṭiyil toḻutaṉaḷ māṟṟi
maṇṇaka maṭantiyi 2mayakkuoḻip paṉaḷpōl
taṇṇīr teḷittut taṉkiyāl taṭavi,
kumari vāḻiyiṉ kuruttuakam virittu īṅku
amutam uṇka, aṭikaḷ!
eṉa vēṇṭiṉaḷ. kōvalaṉum iṉitu avvuṇavi uṇṭaṉaṉ. ivartam nalloḻukkattik kaṇṭa aiyi mutalāyiṉōr mikka viyappuk koṇṭu,
āyar pāṭiyil acōtipeṟ ṟeṭutta
3pūvip putumalar vaṇṇaṉ kollō
nalamu tuṇṇum nampi; īṅku,
palvaḷit tōḷiyum, paṇṭunam kulattut
4toḻuni yāṟṟiṉuḷ tūmaṇi vaṇṇaṉi
5viḻumam tīrtta viḷakkuk kol!
eṉa vimmitattōṭu viḷampi makiḻntaṉar. itaṟkuḷ uṇavu koṇṭa kōvalaṉ iṉitiruppak kaṇṇaki, “ammeṉ 6tiriyalōṭu aṭikkāy īntaṉaḷ.” avaḷ ceyyum arumpaṇikaḷik kaṇṭa kōvalaṉ neñcu kalaṅki, palapaṭa niṉintu, avaḷit taṉ arukil aḻittu irutti, “nallāy, kallum muḷḷum kāṭum ceṟinta aruvaḻikaḷik kaṭattaṟku niṉ mellaṭikaḷ poruntuvaṉa alla; iruppiṉum nī eṉṉuṭaṉ inta neṭuvaḻi vantataṟku em mutukuravar ettuṇit tuyaram eytiṉarō! iv vaḷaviṟkum kāraṇam teriyēṉ; māyamō, aṉṟi valviṉiyō, yāṉ yāvatum aṟiyēṉ, yāṉ itu kāṟum 1poccāppuṇṭu, poruḷuri ikaḻntu, tēṟṟā oḻukkat tāl tī neṟippaṭṭēṉ; eṉakku iṉi naṉṉeṟiyuṇṭō?
irumutu kuravar ēvalum piḻittēṉ;
2ciṟumutuk kuṟivikkuc ciṟumiyum ceytēṉ;
3vaḻuveṉum pārēṉ; mānakar maruṅkuīṇṭu
eḻukeṉa eḻuntāy; eṉceytaṉi!
eṉak kalaṅkik kūṟiṉāṉāka, avaṟkuk kaṇṇaki nallāḷum tāṉ ciṟu taṭiyum nikaḻttātu uṭaṉpōntaṟkuk kāraṇam kūṟuvāḷāy,
aṟavōrk kaḷittalum 4antaṇa rōmpalum
tuṟavōrkku etirtalum tollōr ciṟappiṉ
viruntuetir kōṭalum iḻanta eṉṉi,
taṅkaḷ peṟṟōr aṉpu ciṟantu aruḷmoḻiyāṟ kalantu pārāṭṭiṉar. avarkaṭku varuttamuṇṭākākavāṟu yāṉum taṅkaḷ pirivāl uḷatāya varuttattatip puṟattē ciṟitum kāṭṭātu karantu oḻukiṉēṉ. atu tāṉum avarkaṭku varuttamē tantatu, nīvirum 5pōṟṟā oḻukkamē purintu oḻukiṉīr. taṅkaḷpāl māṟṟāta uḷḷamuṭiyē ṉātalāl, yāṉ uṭaṉpaṭṭup pōn tēṉ” eṉṟaṉaḷ. eṉṟalum kōvalaṉ taṉ pōṟṟā voḻukkattiṉ puṉmiyum, kaṇṇakiyiṉ māṟṟā uḷḷattiṉ māṇpiyum niṉintu,
1kuṭimutaṟ cuṟṟamum kuṟṟiḷi yōrum
2aṭiyōr pāṅkum āyamum nīṅki,
nāṇamum maṭaṉum nallō rēttum
3pēṇiya kaṟpum peruntuṇi yāka
eṉṉoṭu pōntu eṉtuyar kaḷinta
poṉṉē! koṭiyē! puṉipūṅ kōtāy!
4nāṇiṉ pāvāy! 5nīḷnila viḷakkē!
kaṟpiṉ koḻuntē! 6poṟpiṉ celvi!
“nī niṉ kāṟ cilampukaḷuḷ oṉṟik koṭu; ataṉik koṇṭu pōy yāṉ viṟṟuvaruvēṉ; atuvariyil nī ciṟitum mayaṅkaṟka” eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ. kaṇṇakiyum uṭaṉē ataṉi avaṉ kiyiṟ koṭuppa, avaṉ ataṉik koṇṭa aritiṟ pirintu pōyiṉaṉ. avaṉi aṟiyātē neñcu kalaṅkiṟṟu; kaṇkaḷil nīr tatumpiṟṟu. ataṉik kaṇṇakiyō, piṟarō, aṟiyātapaṭi maṟittukkoṇṭu collat toṭaṅkiya kōvalaṉ, āycciyar teruvē pōkiṉṟavaṉ, vaḻiyil, 7imil ēṟu oṉṟu etilē taṉṉip pāya varak kaṇṭu vilakic ceṉṟāṉ. atu tīkkuṟi eṉpatu iṭiyar vaḻakku. ataṉāl, vaṇikaṉākiya kōvalaṉ ataṉip poruṭpaṭuttātu āyar cēri kaṭantu, palvēṟu terukkaḷit tāṇṭip pīṭikit teruvi aṭintāṉ. aṅkē, nuṇviṉik kollar oru nūṟu pēr piṉ vara, poṟkollaṉ oruvaṉ varak kaṇṭāṉ kōvalaṉ. avaṉi aṇuki, “aiya, kāvalaṉ tēvikkut takutiyāyatoru kāṟcilampu eṉpāl uḷatu; ataṉi nī viliyiṭṭut taruviyō?” eṉṟu kōvalaṉ kēṭka, ak kollaṉ, “aṭiyēṉ aṟiyēṉ; āyiṉum, yāṉ vēntar 1muṭi mutaṟ kalaṉkaḷ camippēṉ” eṉṟu kikūppit toḻutu ēttik kūṟiṉaṉ. kōvalaṉ, tāṉ koṇṭu ceṉṟa cilampik kāṭṭiṉaṉ.
cittirac cilampiṉ ceyviṉi yellām
poyttoḻiṟ kollaṉ 2purintuṭaṉ nōkkik
kōpperun tēvikku allati iccilampu
3yāppuṟa villi eṉamuṉ pōntu,
viṟalmiku vēntaṟku viḷampiyāṉ vara, eṉ
ciṟukuṭil aṅkaṇ irumiṉ nīreṉa
moḻintāṉ. kōvalaṉum avvāṟē ceṉṟu, avaṉatu maṉikkarukirunta 4kōyilil iruntāṉ. poṟ kollaṉ, cilampu koṇṭu cellumpōtu taṉakkuḷḷē,
karantuyāṉ koṇṭa 5kālaṇi, īṅkup
parantu veḷip paṭāmuṉṉam, maṉṉaṟkup
6pulampeyar putuvaṉil pōkkuvaṉ, yāṉ

kūṭal makaḷir 7āṭal tōṟṟamum,
8pāṭal pakutiyum, 9paṇṇiṉ payaṅkaḷum,
kāvalaṉ uḷḷam kavarntaṉa, eṉṟu, taṉ
ūṭal uḷḷam 1uḷkarantu oḷittu,
2talinōy varuttam taṉmēl iṭṭu,
pulantu nīṅkiṉaḷ. ataṉi āṟṟāta aracaṉ amiccar kuḻuvili runtu nīṅki, ēṉi makaḷir puṭicūḻa araciyiṉ irukkikkuc ceṉṟu ataṉ vāyili yaṭintāṉ. an niliyil poṟ kollaṉ pukuntu, aracaṉ tiruvaṭiyil vīḻntu vaṇaṅki,
3kaṉṉakam iṉṟiyum 4kavikkōl iṉṟiyum
5tuṉṉiya mantiram tuṇiyeṉak koṇṭu

kōyiṟ cilampu koṇṭa kaḷvaṉ
kalleṉ pērūrk kāvalark karantueṉ
7cillic ciṟukuṭil akattirunta tāṉ

viṉiviḷi kāla mātaliṉ, yāvatum
8ciṉiyalar vēmpaṉ tērā ṉāki
ūrkāp pāḷarik kūvi, īṅkueṉ
tāḻpūṅ kōti taṉkāṟ cilampu
9kaṉṟiya kaḷvaṉ kiya tākiṉ
koṉṟu accilampu koṇarka īṅku
eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭāṉ. poṟkollaṉ, ūrk kāppāḷar piṉvara, kōvalaṉ irunta iṭattiṟkup pōntu, avaṉukku avarkaḷik kāṭṭi, “ivarkaḷ maṉṉavaṉ ēvalāl cilampu kāṇa vantavar” eṉṟāṉ. kōvalaṉ cilampiṉik kāṭṭi ataṉ ceytikaḷiyum kūṟiṉaṉ. vantavaruḷ, cilar, kōvalaṉatu tōṟṟamum, moḻiyum, oḻukkamum kaṇṭu, “1ilakkaṇa muṟimiyiṉ iruntōṉ, kolippaṭu makaṉ alaṉ” eṉṟu iyampiṉar. uṭaṉē poṟkollaṉ, nakittu, avarkaḷ maṉam koḷḷumāṟu takka cāṉṟukaḷāl uṇmi kāṭṭi uṟuti ceyya muṟpaṭṭup pala kūṟaluṟṟāṉ: mutalil,
mantiram, teyvam, maruntē, nimittam,
tantiram, iṭaṉē, kālam, karuvieṉṟu
eṭṭuṭaṉ aṉṟē, 2iḻukkuṭi marapiṉ

mantiram nāviṭi vaḻuttuva rāyiṉ
3intira kumarariṉ yāmkāṇ kuvamō!

maruntiṉ namkaṇ mayakkuva rāyiṉ,
5iruntōm peyarum iṭaṉumār uṇṭō?

pukaṟkilar arumporuḷ vantukip pukutiṉum;

kālam karuti avarporuḷ kiyuṟiṉ
4mēlō rāyiṉum vilakkalum uṇṭō?
5karuvi koṇṭuavar arumporuḷ kiyuṟiṉ
irunila maruṅkil yār kāṇkiṟpār?
iravē pakalē eṉṟuiraṇṭu illi;
6karaviṭam kēṭpiṉōr pukaliṭam illi;
eṉṟu muṟiyākac colli, kaḷvaratu aruñceyaṟkuc cāṉṟāka, teṉṉavaṉ tampiyākiya iḷaṅkō vēntaṉ tōḷilaṇinta vayira āratti oru kaḷvaṉ tiruṭiyatākap poy puṉintu kēṭpōr uṭkum viyappum koḷḷumāṟu kūṟiṉaṉ. ivaṟṟi uṭaṉiruntu kēṭṭōruḷ oruvaṉ, tāṉum oru kaḷavu nikaḻcci kūṟi,
arituivar ceyti; alikkum vēntaṉum;
uriyatuoṉṟu urimiṉ 7uṟupaṭi yīreṉa
viṉaviṉaṉ; appōḻtil, kallāk kaḷimakaṉoruvaṉ taṉ ki vāḷi uruvi vīciṉaṉ; atu kōvalaṉatu kaḻuttil kuṟukkē vīḻntu ūṭaṟuttatu;
puṇṇumiḻ kuruti poḻintu 8uṭaṉ parappa,
maṇṇaka maṭanti 9vāṉtuyar kūrak
kāvalaṉ ceṅkōl 1vaḷiiya vīḻntaṉaṉ
kōvalaṉ.
“kātaliyik koṇṭu, kavuntiyoṭu kūṭi,
mātarikkuk kāṭṭi, maṉiyiṉ akaṉṟupōyk,
kōtil iṟivaṉatu kūṭaṟkaṇ, kōvalaṉceṉṟu
ētam uṟutal 2viṉi.”
- tol.col.teyva.mēṟkōḷ.

17. āycciyar kuravi
karuppam
pāṇṭiyaṉ kōyilil kāli muracu muḻaṅkiṟṟu; atu kēṭṭa mātari, “iṉṟu araṇmaṉikku neyyaḷakkum muṟi namakku vantuḷatu; ātalāl viriya varuka” eṉattaṉ makaḷ aiyiyi aḻittukkoṇṭē kaṭikayiṟum mattum koṇṭu vantāḷ. vantavaḷ piriyiṭṭa kuṭappāl tōyāmal  iruppati yum, veṇṇey urukāmiyum kaṇṭu añci, “nikaḻvatu ētō oṉṟu uṇṭu” eṉṟu coṉṉāḷ. ataṉmēl,
1koḷu
kuṭattup pāl uṟiyāmi(yālum), 2kuvi imilēṟṟiṉ maṭakkaṇṇīr cōrta(lālum), uṟiyil veṇṇey urukāmi (yālum), 3maṟi muṭaṅki āṭāmi(yālum), māṉ maṇi nilattu aṟṟu vīḻta(lālum), varuvatōr tuṉpam uṇṭu eṉa (mātari taṉ makaḷ aiyikkuc colli, avaḷi nōkki) maṉam mayaṅkātē; maṇṇil mātarkku aṇiyākiya kaṇṇakiyum kāṇa, āyarpāṭi yil eru maṉṟattu māyavaṉuṭaṉ 4tammuṉ āṭiya vāla cariti nāṭakaṅkaḷil, vēl neṭuṅkaṇ piññiyōṭu āṭiya kuravi āṭutum yām, kaṟavi kaṉṟu tuyar nīṅkuka eṉa, eṉṟāḷ (mātari).
1eṭuttukkāṭṭu - cuṭṭu.
2neṟṟic cekili aṭarttāṟku uriya, ip
poṟṟoṭi mātarāḷ tōḷ.
3nuṇpoṟi veḷḷi aṭarttāṟkē ākum, ip
peṇkoṭi mātartaṉ tōḷ.
veṉṟi maḻaviṭi ūrntāṟku uriyaḷik
4koṉṟiyam pūṅkuḻa lāḷ.
5peyarīṭu
āya makaḷir eḻuvari niṟutti, ivvēṟṟi ūrntāṉukku im makaḷ uriyaḷ eṉa eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyataṟkēṟpa, avar eḻu variyum vaṭṭamāka niṟutti, mēṟkiliruntu, ovvoruttikkum kural, tuttam, kikkiḷi, uḻi, iḷi, viḷari, tāram eṉap peyariṭṭaṉar. ivvāṟu peyar peṟṟa makaḷiruḷ kural eṉpāḷi māyavaṉ eṉṟum, iḷi eṉpāḷip palatēvaṉ eṉṟum, tuttam eṉṟa peyaruṭiyāḷip piṉṉi eṉṟum koṇṭaṉar. ataṉ piṉ, māyavaṉ pakkattē piṉṉiyāṉa tuttamum tāramum niṉṟaṉar; palatēvaṉic cēra, uḻiyum viḷariyum niṉṟaṉar; kikkiḷi piṉṉikku iṭappakkattum, viḷari tārattukku valappakkattum niṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟē yāḻiṭattum eḻuvaki narampukaḷum iyikkappeṟṟaṉa. in niliyil piṉṉiyiṉ iṭattē mātari niṉṟu, taṉṉiyē piṉṉi eṉak karuti viyappuṟṟāḷ.
1kūttuḷ paṭutal
piṉṉar, im makaḷir eḻuvarum vaṭṭamākak kikōttu niṟpa, kuraliṭattē niṉṟa māyavaṉākiya āya makaḷ,
kollip puṉattuk 2kuruntocittāṟ pāṭutum
3mullittīm pāṇi eṉṟāḷ.
iṉi, ivaruḷ, pāṭṭeṭuppavaḷ kural manta curamākavum, iḷi cama curamākavum niṟka, tuttam valiya curamākap pāṭiṉaḷ. viḷari manta curamāka eṭuttu, tuttamākiyavaṭkut tuṇiyāyp paṟṟip pāṭiṉaḷ. ivarkaḷ pāṭiya pāṭṭu.
pāṭṭu
 kaṉṟu 4kuṇilāk kaṉiutirtta māyavaṉ
iṉṟunam āṉuḷ varumēl avaṉvāyil
5koṉṟiyam tīṅkuḻal kēḷāmō tōḻī.
kolliyam cāral kuruntocitta māyavaṉ
ellinam āṉuḷ varumēl avaṉvāyil
6mulliyam tīṅkuḻal kēḷāmō tōḻī.
kuravi makaḷir kūṟṟu
ivvāṟu pāṭi āṭiya makaḷir piṉṉippirāṭṭiyiṉ ciṟappi eṭuttu ōtutaṟku muṟpaṭṭuc cila pāṭiṉar.
vañcam ceytāṉ 7toḻuṉip puṉaluḷ
neñcam kavarntāḷ niṟi8eṉ kōyām;
neñcam kavarntāḷ niṟiyum vaḷiyum
vañcam ceytāṉ vaṭivueṉ kōyām.
tiyal 1kaliyum vaḷiyum iḻantē
2kiyil oḷittāḷ mukameṉ kōyām;
kiyil oḷittāḷ mukamkaṇṭu aḻuṅki
3miyal uḻantāṉ vaṭivueṉ kōyām.
4oṉṟaṉ pakuti
5katirtikiri yālmaṟitta kaṭalvaṇṇaṉ iṭattuḷāḷ,
6matipuriyum naṟumēṉit tammuṉōṉ valattuḷāḷ,
7potiaviḻ malarkkūntal piṉṉicīr puṟaṅkāppār,
8mutumaṟitēr nārataṉār muntimuṟi narampuuḷarvār.
9āṭunarp pukaḻtal

	varivaḷikkip piṉṉiyoṭum

	kuḻaṟkōti 11puṟañcōra,

	acōtiyār toḻutēttat
12tāterumaṉ ṟattāṭum
	kuraviyō takavuṭittē.

maṉṉaṉ vaḷavaṉ matiṟpukār vāḻvēntaṉ

18. tuṉpa māli
ivvaṇṇam, āṉirikaṭkuṇṭāya tīkkuṟi kaṇṭu kuravi yāṭiya āycciyar viyikku nīrāṭap pōyiṉar; mātariyum nīrāṭip, pūvum pukiyum koṇṭu tirumāliṉ tiruvaṭi paravap pōṉāḷ. appōtu an nakarkkuc ceṉṟirunta āyarmakaḷ orutti, kōvalaṉukkuṟṟa tīṅku kaṇṭu ataṉit terivikka mikka virivāka vantāḷ. varum pōtu, āycciyar kūṭṭattē kaṇṇaki niṟpa, avaḷatu aḻakum iḷamiyum, taṉimiyum kaṇṭu neñcaḻintu āṟṟātu, vāyāṟ collamāṭṭātu, nīrāṭip pōnta makaḷirkkuk kuṟippākak kūṟiṉaḷ. ataṉ uṇmi kaṇṇakikkut terintatu. uṭaṉē,
1ellāvō,
kātalaṟ kāṇkilēṉ, kalaṅki 2nōy kimmikum;
3ūtuli tōṟka uyirkkum eṉneñcu, aṉṟē;
ūtuli tōṟka uyirkkum eṉneñcu āyiṉ,
4ētilār coṉṉatu evaṉ vāḻiyō, tōḻī.
5tañcamō tōḻī, talivaṉvarak kāṇēṉ;
vañcamō uṇṭu, mayaṅkum eṉneñcu aṉṟē;
vañcamō uṇṭu, mayaṅkum eṉneñcu, āyiṉ,
6eñcalār coṉṉatu evaṉvāḻiyō, tōḻī.
eṉṟu kaṇṇaki kataṟik kūṟiṉaḷ. atu kēṭṭa avvāyar makaḷ pēravalam uṟṟu,
aracuṟi kōyil aṇiyār 1ñeciḻam
2kariyāmal vāṅkiya kaḷvaṉām eṉṟē;
kariyāmal vāṅkiya kaḷvaṉām eṉṟē
3kurikaḻal mākkaḷ 4kolikuṟit taṉarē;
eṉṟu coṉṉāḷ. ataṉik kēṭṭāḷō illiyō, kaṇṇaki,
poṅki eḻuntāḷ, viḻuntāḷ, 5poḻikatirt
tiṅkaḷ mukilōṭum cēṇnilam koṇṭeṉac
ceṅkaṇ civappa aḻutāḷ; taṉ 6kēḷvaṉi
7eṅkaṇā eṉṉā iṉintu ēṅki 8māḻkuvāḷ.
iṉpuṟutam kaṇavā
	9iṭareri akammūḻkat
tuṉpuṟuvaṉa nōṟṟut
	tuyaruṟu makaḷirippōl
10maṉpati alartūṟṟa
	maṉṉavaṉ tavaṟiḻippa
aṉpaṉi aḻantēṉyāṉ
	11avalamkoṇṭu aḻivalō.
tammuṟu peruṅkaṇavaṉ
	taḻaleri akamūḻkak
1kimmikūr tuṟimūḻkum
	2kavaliya makaḷirippōl,
3cemmiyiṉ ikantakōl
	teṉṉavaṉ tavaṟiḻippa,
immiyum 4iciorīi
	iṉintu ēṅki aḻivalō.
ivvaṇṇam vāyviṭṭuk kataṟip pulampiya kaṇṇaki, ēṉimakaḷir aṉivariyum aḻittu, “āyarmakaḷirē, kēṇmiṉ” eṉṟu colli, vāṉattic cuṭṭip pakalavaṉi aḻittu,
5pāytiri vēlip paṭuporuḷ nī aṟiti;
6kāykatirc celvaṉē! kaḷvaṉō eṉkaṇavaṉ?
eṉṟāḷ. uṭaṉē, aṅkē iruntavar aṉivarum teḷiyak kēṭkumpaṭiyāka,
kaḷvaṉō allaṉ; karuṅkayalkaṇ mātarāy!
7oḷḷeri uṇṇum ivvūr
eṉṟu vāṉattē oru kural eḻuntu icittatu.
19. ūrcūḻ vari
ivvaṇṇam pakalōṉ, “niṉ kaṇavaṉ kaḷvaṉ allaṉ” eṉṟu coṉṉatu kēṭṭatum, kaṇṇaki, avviṭattu nillātu, taṉṉiṭattē irunta maṟṟic cilampik kiyil ēntikkoṇṭu,
muṟiyil aracaṉtaṉ ūriruntu vāḻum
niṟiyuṭip pattiṉip peṇṭirkāḷ, 1ītuoṉṟu;
paṭṭēṉ paṭāta tuyaram; paṭukāli
uṟṟēṉ 2uṟātatu uṟuvaṉē, ītuoṉṟu;
kaḷvaṉō allaṉ kaṇavaṉ, eṉ kāṟcilampu
koḷḷum vilipporuṭṭāl 3koṉṟārē, īḻoṉṟu;
eṉṟu araṟṟip piṉṉum kūṟuvāḷ, “im makaḷir kaṇṇetirē yāṉ ippōtē ceṉṟu eṉ kātaṟ kaṇavaṉik kāṇpēṉ; kaṇṭu, avaṉ vāyāl tāṉ tītilaṉ eṉṟa nalluriyik kēṭpēṉ; kēḷātu pōvēṉēl, nīvir, nummi yāṉ vaṟitē paḻitūṟṟiṉēṉ eṉṟu eṉṉi ikaḻmiṉ” eṉṟu collip pulampiṉāḷ. ivvāṟu aḻutu cellum ivaḷi maturi nakarattu makkaḷ kaṇṭu kaḻipēr irakkam koṇṭu,
1kaḷiyāta tuṉpam
	ikkārikikkuk kāṭṭi
vaḷiyāta ceṅkōl
	vaḷintatu itueṉkol?
maṉṉavar maṉṉaṉ
	2matikkuṭi vāḷvēntaṉ
teṉṉavaṉ 3koṟṟam
	citintatu itueṉkol?
cempoṉ cilampuoṉṟu
	kiyēnti namporuṭṭāl
4vampap perunteyvam
	vantatu itueṉkol?
5aiyari uṇkaṇ
	aḻutēṅki araṟṟuvāḷ
teyvam uṟṟāḷpōlum
	6takiyaḷ itu eṉkol
eṉpaṉa pala colli ēṅki varuntiṉar. kaṇṇaki, taṉ kaṇavaṉ uṭalik kāṇṭaṟkuc cella, cilar avaṭku avaṉ veṭṭuṇṭu kiṭanta iṭattiyum, avaṉatu uṭaliyum kāṭṭiṉar. kaṇṇaki ataṉik kaṇṭāḷ; ceṅkatirōṉ mēlit ticiyil maṟin tāṉ; melliya iruḷ parantatu. kōvalaṉ vīḻntu kiṭanta iṭam, avaṉ uṭaliliruntu oḻukiya kurutiyāl naṉintu kiṭantatu. kāliyil taṉ kaṇavaṉit taḻuvi, avaṉ tanta māliyit taṉ kūntalil cūṭikkoṇṭa kaṇṇaki, aṉṟu māliyil mey yellām kurutivāra veṭṭuṇṭirunta avaṉik kaṇṭu kāṇāk kaṭuntuyaram kaṇṭāḷ. piṉpu, avaṉit taḻuvi, ōveṉakkataṟi,
eṉuṟu tuyarkaṇṭum 1iṭaruṟum ivaḷeṉṉīr,
2poṉṉuṟu naṟumēṉi 3poṭiāṭik kiṭappatō!
4maṉṉuṟu tuyarceyta 5maṟaviṉi aṟiyātēṟ(ku)
6eṉuṟu viṉikāṇā itueṉa uriyārō!
yārumil maruḷmāli iṭaruṟu tamiyēṉmuṉ,
7tārmali maṇimārpam tarimūḻkik kiṭappatō!
pārmiku paḻitūṟṟap pāṇṭiyaṉ tavaṟiḻippa,
8īrvatōr viṉikāṇā itueṉa uriyārō!
ivvāṟu pulampiṉavaḷ, nāṭṭil ceṅkōl piḻiyā tāyiṉ an nāṭṭil niṟiyuṭiya peṇṭirum, cāṉṟōrum teyvamum iruppar; in nāṭṭil avarkaḷ illiyō allatu uṇṭō eṉpāḷpōlap “peṇṭirum uṇṭu kol? cāṉṟōrum uṇṭu kol? teyvamum uṇṭu kol” eṉac colli aḻutāḷ. piṉpu, avaṉ uṭali eṭuttut taṉ mārpōṭu taḻuviṉapōtu, avaṉ uyir peṟṟāṉpōlat teḷintu niṉṟu, avaḷ mukattit taṭavi, “mikavum 9kaṉṟiyatu” eṉa avaḷ kaṇṇīrik kiyāl tuṭittāṉ. kaṇṇaki avaṉ tiruvaṭiyil vīḻntu vaṇaṅki, avaṉ aṭikaḷik kiyāl paṟṟiṉaḷ. avaṉ tāṉ irunta uṭaliliruntu nīṅkic cūḻa niṉṟa iṟiyavarōṭu tāṉ ōr iṟiyavaṉāy niṉṟu, “uṇkaṇṇāy, nī īṇṭē irukka” eṉac colliviṭṭup pōṉāṉ. kaṇṇaki āṟṟāḷāy,
māyamkol, maṟṟueṉkol,
	1maruṭṭi yatōr teyvamkol
pōyeṅku nāṭukēṉ;
	2poruḷuriyō, ituaṉṟu;
3kāyciṉam taṇintaṉṟik
	kaṇavaṉik kikūṭēṉ;
4tīvēntaṉ taṉikkaṇṭu
	ittiṟamkēṭpal yāṉeṉṟāḷ.
eṉṟu ivvakiyil taṉakkuḷ colliyavaḷ eḻuntu niṉṟāḷ. muṉpu kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattut tāṉ kaṇṭa kaṉavu avaḷ niṉiviṟku vantatu. taṉ kaṇṇīri mīṭṭum tuṭittuk koṇṭu avaḷ pāṇṭivēntaṉ kōyil vāyili yaṭintāḷ.
20. vaḻakkuri kāti

3. vañcik kāṇṭam
24. kuṉṟak kuravi
yāṅkaḷ muṉṉināḷ 1neṭuvēḷ kuṉṟattukkuc ceṉṟu, aṅkē taṅkik “2kuruviyōppiyum, kiḷi kaṭintum, aruviyāṭiyum, 3cuṉikuṭintum” vantukoṇṭiruntēm; atupōtu, am maliyiṭattē oru vēṅki marattiṉkīḻ oru naṅki niṟpak kaṇṭēm; avaḷi aṇuki, “4vaḷḷi pōlvīr, nummik kaṇṭa em maṉam naṭuṅka, muliyiḻantu niṟkiṉṟa nīvir yāvirō?” eṉa viṉaviṉēm. avaḷ, emmic ciṟitum muṉiyātu,
“maṇamaturiyōṭu aracu kēṭuṟa
	valviṉi vantu urutta kāli,
kaṇavaṉi aṅku iḻantu pōnta
	kaṭuviṉi yēṉ, yāṉ”
eṉṟāḷ. itu kēṭṭatum yāṅkaḷ mikavum añci, irukiyum kūppi vaṇaṅki niṉṟēm. akkāliyil, vāṉavar pōntu malarmāri poḻintu, kuṉṟavar aṉivarum kaṇṭu viyantu niṟpa, avaḷi avaḷuṭiya koḻunaṉuṭaṉ vāṉulaku koṇṭu pōyiṉar. “ivaḷ pōlum oru periya teyvam nam kulattukku illi; ātalāl, ivaḷi ivvēṅkiyiṉ kīḻē 5teyvam koḷḷa vēṇṭum”
eṉṟu eṇṇi, em ciṟu kuṭiyil vāḻvārkku em eṇṇattit terivittēm.

cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ, vañcimā nakarattē ilavanti veḷḷi māṭattiṉkaṇ taṉtēvi iḷaṅkō vēṇmāḷuṭaṉ iṉitirukkum orunāḷ, “mañcucūḻ cōli malivaḷam kāṇkuvam” eṉak karuti urimi makaḷirum, ēṉi āyattavarum orupuṭiyē cūḻntuvara, paṭivīrarum piṟarum maṟṟorupuṭiyē aṇipeṟavara, vañci nakaratti viṭṭu nīṅkic ceṉṟaṉaṉ. ac celavu,

iruntatu. ivvāṟu ceṉṟa kuṭṭuvaṉ maliccarivil, tirumāliṉ mārpil viḷaṅkum muttumāli pōlak kiṭantu taṉ teḷinta nīrāl iṉiya kāṭci vaḻaṅkum pēriyāṟṟiṉ maṇal paranta aṭikariyi aṭintāṉ. āṅku, nākam, cantaṉam mutaliya palavaki maraṅkaḷ ceṟintiruntaṉa. naṟumaṇam kamaḻum pūkkaḷ tēṉmikkut tikaḻntaṉa. at tēṉi yuṇṇum vaṇṭum, ñimirum, matukaramum moyttu iṉitu pāṭiṉa. mēlum,
